+    def _poll_comparison_queue(self) -> None:
+        if not self._comparison_running or self._comparison_queue is None:
+            return
+
+        while True:
+            try:
+                event = self._comparison_queue.get_nowait()
+            except queue.Empty:
+                break
+
+            if isinstance(event, ComparisonWorkerProgress):
+                self._set_status(event.message)
+                continue
+
+            if isinstance(event, ComparisonWorkerSuccess):
+                self._handle_comparison_success(event.result)
+                return
+
+            if isinstance(event, ComparisonWorkerFailure):
+                self._handle_comparison_failure(event)
+                return
+
+        if self._comparison_running:
+            self._root.after(100, self._poll_comparison_queue)
+
+    def _finish_comparison_session(self) -> None:
+        self._comparison_running = False
+        self._comparison_queue = None
+        self._comparison_thread = None
+        self._generate_btn.configure(state="normal")

+    def _handle_comparison_success(self, result) -> None:
+        raw_count = len(result.deviations)
+        actionable_deviations = [deviation for deviation in result.deviations if is_actionable(deviation)]
+        compare_elapsed = result.summary.execution_time_seconds or round(time.perf_counter() - self._comparison_started_at, 2)
+
+        self._sheets_text.set(str(result.summary.total_sheets_compared))
+        self._cells_text.set(str(result.summary.total_cells_compared))
+        self._raw_deviations_text.set(str(raw_count))
+        self._actionable_deviations_text.set(str(len(actionable_deviations)))
+        self._time_text.set(f"Comparison: {compare_elapsed:.2f} s")
+        self._output_text.set("Not saved yet")
+        self._raw_count_for_preview = raw_count
+        self._populate_preview(actionable_deviations)
 
+        try:
             should_save = messagebox.askyesno(
                 "Save Deviation Report",
                 f"Comparison finished with {len(actionable_deviations)} actionable deviations from {raw_count} raw differences.\n\n"

# Train Type Data Preparation Tool

A Windows desktop application that generates a lean Excel file containing
**only the "Train Type" sheet**, pre-filled with values for a selected train type.
The output is intended to be copied manually into a large existing Excel workbook.

For Train Type generation the app now rebuilds the worksheet directly from one
of three JSON variants selected by the user: `Generic`, `Heavy Diesel`, `Light Diesel`.

The application also includes a local workbook comparison feature that compares
a project workbook against an embedded standard workbook and generates a deviation report.

> **The Train Type generator does not read or modify user Excel files.**
> The comparison feature reads user-selected workbooks locally in read-only
> mode and never modifies or saves them.

---

## Directory Structure

```
data_prep_tool/
│
├── app/
│   ├── comparison/                 # Embedded standard compare pipeline and report generation
│   ├── ui/
│   │   ├── main_window.py          # Tkinter shell
│   │   └── compare_tab.py          # Compare tab UI and save flow
│   ├── services/
│   │   ├── rebuild.py              # Primary Train Type generator from JSON variants
│   │   ├── data_update_service.py  # Reads/writes metadata.json (generation log)
│   │   ├── train_type_service.py   # Legacy compatibility path for value lookup
│   │   └── excel_generator.py      # Legacy compatibility path for template writes
│   ├── repositories/
│   │   ├── base_repository.py
│   │   ├── local_json_repository.py
│   │   └── sharepoint_repository.py
│   ├── models/
│   │   └── train_type.py
│   ├── runtime_assets.py           # Copies bundled runtime files into the writable app folder
│   └── config.py
├── data/
│   ├── train_type_variants/
│   │   ├── Generic.json
│   │   ├── HeavyDiesel.json
│   │   └── LightDiesel.json
│   ├── standards/7_7_2/
│   │   ├── standard_7_7_2_precalculated_UNLOCKED.xlsm
│   │   └── standard_metadata.json
│   ├── train_type_values.json      # Legacy compatibility data
│   ├── excel_mapping.json          # Legacy compatibility mapping
│   └── metadata.json               # Generation history log (auto-updated)
├── legacy/
│   └── developer_utility/

├── templates/
├── tests/
├── main.py
├── requirements.txt
└── README.md
```

---

## Prerequisites

- Python 3.11 or newer
- Windows (required for the Tkinter UI and PyInstaller `.exe` target)

---

## Setup

### 1. Install dependencies

```bash
cd data_prep_tool
pip install -r requirements.txt
```

### 2. Create the Excel template (run once)

```bash
python legacy/developer_utility/create_template.py
```

This writes `templates/train_type_template.xlsx`. Only run it again if you need
to reset the template to its default layout.

### 3. Run the application

```bash
python main.py
```

---

## Configuration

### `app/config.py`

| Setting | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `SHAREPOINT_TRAIN_TYPE_VALUES_URL` | HTTPS URL to `train_type_values.json` on SharePoint |
| `SHAREPOINT_EXCEL_MAPPING_URL` | HTTPS URL to `excel_mapping.json` on SharePoint |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` | HTTP timeout before falling back to local JSON |

Update the SharePoint URLs before deploying. If SharePoint is unreachable,
the app silently falls back to the local `/data` files and logs which source was used.

---

## Data files

### `data/train_type_variants/*.json`

Each variant file contains a full worksheet definition (`sheet_name`, `cells`,
`column_widths`, `row_heights`, `merged_cells`).

When the user clicks **Generate Excel**, the selected variant is rebuilt into
a new workbook and saved to the location chosen in the save dialog.

### `data/train_type_values.json`

Defines all available train types and their parameter values:

```json
{
  "train_types": {
    "generic": {
      "max_speed_kmh": 160,
      "traction_type": "Electric",
      ...
    }
  }
}
```

Add a new train type by adding a new key under `"train_types"`.

### `data/excel_mapping.json`

Maps each field name to a specific sheet and cell in the template:

```json
{
  "mappings": [
    { "sheet": "Train Type", "cell": "B2", "field": "max_speed_kmh" },
    ...
  ]
}
```

To change where a value is written in the Excel output, edit this file —
no Python code changes are required.

## Compare Workbook Against Standard

The application includes a second tab for deterministic workbook comparison.

### Inputs

- Project Workbook (`.xlsm`) selected by the user
- Embedded standard workbook loaded automatically from `data/standards/7_7_2/standard_7_7_2_precalculated_UNLOCKED.xlsm`

### Output

- `Workbook_Deviations.xlsx`
- Worksheets in order: `Summary`, `List of Project Deviations`, `All Cell Differences`
- Both deviation worksheets use the columns `Change No`, `Sheet`, `Cell`, `Standard Value`, `Project Value`, `Parameter`, `Rule`
- `List of Project Deviations` is the actionable subset
- `All Cell Differences` contains the full raw comparison output

### Comparison rules

- Compares cell values only.
- Ignores formatting, macros, VBA, colors, borders, comments, row heights, column widths, and workbook metadata.
- Skips sheets configured in `data/standards/7_7_2/standard_metadata.json` and sheets hidden in either workbook.
- Treats whitespace-trimmed strings and empty values consistently.
- Uses Passive Rules enrichment from the embedded standard workbook when available.
- Supports password-protected workbooks using `IDDP_Protection_842` when needed.

The app shows a preview of actionable deviations, then asks where to save `Workbook_Deviations.xlsx`.
The standard workbook uses manual calculation mode, so Excel may need a manual recalc after opening the source workbook.

---

## Building a standalone `.exe`

```powershell
.\build.ps1
```

To use a custom interpreter:

```powershell
.\build.ps1 -Python "C:\Program Files\Python314\python.exe"
```

The resulting executable is at `dist\TrainTypeDataPrepTool.exe`.

> **Important:** The `data/` and `templates/` directories are bundled inside the
> `.exe` via `--add-data`. On first launch the app copies those defaults into a
> writable runtime folder next to the executable, so `metadata.json` and logs
> can be created safely.

If you want to run PyInstaller manually:

```powershell
pyinstaller --noconfirm --clean --onefile --windowed `
  --name TrainTypeDataPrepTool `
  --add-data "data;data" `
  --add-data "templates;templates" `
  main.py
```

The comparison feature requires the optional `msoffcrypto-tool` dependency so
password-protected workbooks can be opened locally when needed.

Application logging is written to `application.log` in the application folder.

---

## How it works

1. The primary Generate tab uses `RebuildService` plus `data/train_type_variants/*.json` to rebuild the selected variant.
2. On startup the app prepares runtime assets in the writable app folder.
3. The user selects `Generic`, `Heavy Diesel`, or `Light Diesel`.
4. A Save dialog asks where to write the output file.
5. A generation log entry is appended to `data/metadata.json`.

### Mapping modes

The app supports two ways to map values into the workbook:

1. `mappings` list: explicit `sheet + cell + field` entries.
2. `parameter_column_mapping`: lookup by parameter name column (for large Train Type sheets).

Current configuration uses `parameter_column_mapping` and writes values by matching
JSON keys against parameter names in column `D` and writing to column `E`.

---

## Layering rules (do not violate)

| Layer | Allowed dependencies |
|---|---|
| `ui/` | `services/`, comparison orchestration |
| `comparison/` | stdlib, openpyxl, local comparison helpers |
| `services/` | `models/`, `repositories/`, `config` |
| `repositories/` | `models/`, `config` |
| `models/` | stdlib only |
| `config.py` | stdlib only |

`TrainTypeService`, `ExcelGenerator`, `train_type_values.json`, `excel_mapping.json`, and `SharePointRepository` are retained compatibility components, not the primary GUI generation path.

param(
  [string]$Python = "python"
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

$pythonCommand = Get-Command $Python -ErrorAction Stop
$pythonPath = $pythonCommand.Source
if (-not $pythonPath) {
  $pythonPath = $pythonCommand.Path
}
if (-not $pythonPath) {
  throw "Unable to resolve Python interpreter: $Python"
}

Write-Host "Using Python interpreter: $pythonPath"
& $pythonPath --version

& $pythonPath -m pip install -r requirements.txt

& $pythonPath -m PyInstaller `
  --noconfirm `
  --clean `
  --onefile `
  --windowed `
  --name TrainTypeDataPrepTool `
  --add-data 'data;data' `
  --add-data 'templates;templates' `
  main.py

Write-Host 'Build complete. Check the dist\ folder for TrainTypeDataPrepTool.exe.'

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import TextIO

OUTPUT_FILE = "repo_full_export.txt"

IGNORE_DIRS = {
    ".git", ".venv", "venv", "env", "__pycache__", ".idea", ".vscode",
    ".pytest_cache", ".mypy_cache", ".ruff_cache", ".tox", ".nox",
    "build", "dist", "logs", "node_modules", "site-packages",
}

IGNORE_FILES = {
    OUTPUT_FILE,
    "application.log",
    "Thumbs.db",
    ".DS_Store",
}

BINARY_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".xls", ".xlsx", ".xlsm", ".xlsb", ".xltx", ".xltm",
    ".doc", ".docx", ".ppt", ".pptx", ".pdf",
    ".zip", ".7z", ".rar", ".exe", ".dll", ".pyd", ".pyc",
    ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".webp", ".ico", ".bmp",
    ".tif", ".tiff", ".bin", ".db", ".sqlite", ".sqlite3",
}

SEPARATOR_LENGTH = 120


def write_separator(outfile: TextIO, title: str) -> None:
    outfile.write("\n")
    outfile.write("=" * SEPARATOR_LENGTH)
    outfile.write("\n")
    outfile.write(title)
    outfile.write("\n")
    outfile.write("=" * SEPARATOR_LENGTH)
    outfile.write("\n\n")


def format_size(size_bytes: int) -> str:
    if size_bytes < 1024:
        return f"{size_bytes} B"
    if size_bytes < 1024 * 1024:
        return f"{size_bytes / 1024:.2f} KB"
    return f"{size_bytes / (1024 * 1024):.2f} MB"


def calculate_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    hasher = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as infile:
        while True:
            chunk = infile.read(1024 * 1024)
            if not chunk:
                break
            hasher.update(chunk)
    return hasher.hexdigest()


def run_git_command(root_dir: Path, arguments: list[str]) -> str:
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["git", *arguments],
            cwd=root_dir,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="replace",
            check=False,
        )
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return "[Git command is not available on this computer.]"
    except OSError as exc:
        return f"[Git command failed: {exc}]"

    parts: list[str] = []
    if result.stdout.strip():
        parts.append(result.stdout.rstrip())
    if result.stderr.strip():
        parts.extend(["[STDERR]", result.stderr.rstrip()])
    return "\n".join(parts) if parts else "[No output]"


def should_ignore_directory(directory_name: str) -> bool:
    return directory_name in IGNORE_DIRS


def should_ignore_file(path: Path) -> bool:
    if path.name in IGNORE_FILES:
        return True
    lowercase_name = path.name.lower()
    if lowercase_name.startswith("repo_after_") and path.suffix.lower() == ".txt":
        return True
    if lowercase_name.startswith("repo_full_export") and path.suffix.lower() == ".txt":
        return True
    return False


def collect_repository_files(root_dir: Path) -> list[Path]:
    collected_files: list[Path] = []

    for current_root, directories, files in os.walk(root_dir):
        directories[:] = sorted(
            directory
            for directory in directories
            if not should_ignore_directory(directory)
        )
        current_path = Path(current_root)

        for filename in sorted(files):
            file_path = current_path / filename
            if should_ignore_file(file_path):
                continue
            collected_files.append(file_path)

    return collected_files


def looks_binary(path: Path) -> bool:
    if path.suffix.lower() in BINARY_EXTENSIONS:
        return True

    try:
        with path.open("rb") as infile:
            sample = infile.read(8192)
    except OSError:
        return True

    if not sample:
        return False
    if b"\x00" in sample:
        return True

    allowed_controls = {7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 13, 27}
    non_text_count = 0
    for byte in sample:
        ascii_printable = 32 <= byte <= 126
        allowed_control = byte in allowed_controls
        non_ascii = byte >= 128
        if not (ascii_printable or allowed_control or non_ascii):
            non_text_count += 1

    return non_text_count / len(sample) > 0.30


def read_text_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
    encodings = ("utf-8-sig", "utf-8", "utf-16", "cp1250", "cp1252", "latin-1")
    for encoding in encodings:
        try:
            return path.read_text(encoding=encoding), encoding
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            continue
        except OSError as exc:
            return None, f"read error: {exc}"
    return None, "unknown encoding"


def export_repository_information(
    outfile: TextIO,
    root_dir: Path,
    repository_files: list[Path],
) -> None:
    write_separator(outfile, "REPOSITORY EXPORT INFORMATION")
    outfile.write(f"Export date: {datetime.now().isoformat(timespec='seconds')}\n")
    outfile.write(f"Repository root: {root_dir}\n")
    outfile.write(f"Output file: {OUTPUT_FILE}\n")
    outfile.write(f"Discovered files: {len(repository_files)}\n")
    outfile.write(f"Ignored directories: {', '.join(sorted(IGNORE_DIRS))}\n")


def export_git_information(outfile: TextIO, root_dir: Path) -> None:
    write_separator(outfile, "GIT REPOSITORY INFORMATION")
    commands = [
        ("CURRENT COMMIT", ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]),
        ("CURRENT BRANCH", ["branch", "--show-current"]),
        ("GIT STATUS", ["status", "--short", "--untracked-files=all"]),
        ("TRACKED FILES", ["ls-files"]),
        ("LAST 10 COMMITS", ["log", "--oneline", "--decorate", "-10"]),
        ("UNCOMMITTED DIFF", ["diff", "--no-ext-diff"]),
        ("STAGED DIFF", ["diff", "--cached", "--no-ext-diff"]),
    ]
    for title, arguments in commands:
        outfile.write(f"{title}\n")
        outfile.write("-" * 40 + "\n")
        outfile.write(run_git_command(root_dir, arguments))
        outfile.write("\n\n")


def export_repository_structure(
    outfile: TextIO,
    root_dir: Path,
    repository_files: list[Path],
) -> None:
    write_separator(outfile, "REPOSITORY STRUCTURE")

    files_by_parent: dict[Path, list[Path]] = {}
    all_directories: set[Path] = {root_dir}

    for file_path in repository_files:
        files_by_parent.setdefault(file_path.parent, []).append(file_path)
        current_directory = file_path.parent
        while root_dir == current_directory or root_dir in current_directory.parents:
            all_directories.add(current_directory)
            if current_directory == root_dir:
                break
            current_directory = current_directory.parent

    sorted_directories = sorted(
        all_directories,
        key=lambda directory: (
            len(directory.relative_to(root_dir).parts),
            str(directory.relative_to(root_dir)).lower(),
        ),
    )

    for directory in sorted_directories:
        relative_directory = directory.relative_to(root_dir)
        if relative_directory == Path("."):
            level = 0
            display_name = f"{root_dir.name}/"
        else:
            level = len(relative_directory.parts)
            display_name = f"{directory.name}/"

        outfile.write(f"{'    ' * level}{display_name}\n")
        directory_files = sorted(
            files_by_parent.get(directory, []),
            key=lambda item: item.name.lower(),
        )
        for file_path in directory_files:
            try:
                size = file_path.stat().st_size
            except OSError:
                size = 0
            marker = "[BINARY]" if looks_binary(file_path) else "[TEXT]"
            outfile.write(
                f"{'    ' * (level + 1)}{marker} {file_path.name} ({format_size(size)})\n"
            )


def export_text_file_contents(
    outfile: TextIO,
    root_dir: Path,
    repository_files: list[Path],
) -> tuple[int, int]:
    write_separator(outfile, "TEXT FILE CONTENTS")
    exported_count = 0
    failed_count = 0

    for file_path in repository_files:
        if looks_binary(file_path):
            continue

        relative_path = file_path.relative_to(root_dir)
        try:
            size = file_path.stat().st_size
        except OSError:
            size = 0

        content, encoding = read_text_file(file_path)
        write_separator(
            outfile,
            f"FILE: {relative_path} | SIZE: {format_size(size)} | ENCODING: {encoding}",
        )

        if content is None:
            failed_count += 1
            outfile.write(f"[ERROR: Could not read text file: {relative_path}]\n")
            continue

        exported_count += 1
        outfile.write(content)
        if content and not content.endswith("\n"):
            outfile.write("\n")

    return exported_count, failed_count


def export_binary_inventory(
    outfile: TextIO,
    root_dir: Path,
    repository_files: list[Path],
) -> tuple[int, int]:
    write_separator(outfile, "BINARY FILE INVENTORY")
    binary_count = 0
    failed_hash_count = 0

    outfile.write("Binary file contents are not embedded in this text export.\n")
    outfile.write("Each binary file is listed with its path, size and SHA-256 hash.\n")
    outfile.write("Upload relevant Excel reports and workbooks separately.\n\n")

    for file_path in repository_files:
        if not looks_binary(file_path):
            continue

        binary_count += 1
        relative_path = file_path.relative_to(root_dir)
        try:
            size = file_path.stat().st_size
        except OSError:
            size = 0

        try:
            sha256 = calculate_sha256(file_path)
        except OSError as exc:
            sha256 = f"[HASH ERROR: {exc}]"
            failed_hash_count += 1

        outfile.write(f"Path: {relative_path}\n")
        outfile.write(f"Size: {format_size(size)} ({size} bytes)\n")
        outfile.write(f"SHA-256: {sha256}\n")
        outfile.write("-" * 80 + "\n")

    return binary_count, failed_hash_count


def export_summary(
    outfile: TextIO,
    repository_files: list[Path],
    text_count: int,
    text_failures: int,
    binary_count: int,
    binary_hash_failures: int,
) -> None:
    write_separator(outfile, "EXPORT SUMMARY")
    outfile.write(f"Total discovered files: {len(repository_files)}\n")
    outfile.write(f"Text files exported: {text_count}\n")
    outfile.write(f"Text read failures: {text_failures}\n")
    outfile.write(f"Binary files inventoried: {binary_count}\n")
    outfile.write(f"Binary hash failures: {binary_hash_failures}\n")
    success = text_failures == 0 and binary_hash_failures == 0
    outfile.write(f"Export status: {'SUCCESS' if success else 'COMPLETED WITH WARNINGS'}\n")


def export_repository() -> Path:
    root_dir = Path.cwd().resolve()
    output_path = root_dir / OUTPUT_FILE
    repository_files = collect_repository_files(root_dir)

    with output_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as outfile:
        export_repository_information(outfile, root_dir, repository_files)
        export_git_information(outfile, root_dir)
        export_repository_structure(outfile, root_dir, repository_files)
        text_count, text_failures = export_text_file_contents(
            outfile, root_dir, repository_files
        )
        binary_count, binary_hash_failures = export_binary_inventory(
            outfile, root_dir, repository_files
        )
        export_summary(
            outfile,
            repository_files,
            text_count,
            text_failures,
            binary_count,
            binary_hash_failures,
        )

    return output_path


if __name__ == "__main__":
    generated_file = export_repository()
    print()
    print("Repository export completed.")
    print(f"Output file: {generated_file}")
    print()
    print("Upload this text file for repository analysis.")
    print("Upload relevant .xlsx or .xlsm files separately.")

def _get_app_dir() -> str:
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
    return os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))


def _get_resource_dir() -> str:
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return getattr(sys, "_MEIPASS", os.path.dirname(sys.executable))
    return os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

    HEAVY_DIESEL_VARIANT_FILE,
    TRAIN_TYPE_VARIANTS_DIR,

def _copy_if_missing(source_path: str, target_path: str) -> None:
    if os.path.abspath(source_path) == os.path.abspath(target_path):
        return
    if os.path.exists(target_path):
        return
    if not os.path.exists(source_path):
        logger.warning("Bundled asset missing: %s", source_path)
        return

    shutil.copy2(source_path, target_path)
    logger.info("Initialized runtime asset: %s", target_path)


def ensure_runtime_assets() -> None:
    os.makedirs(APP_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(LOGS_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(TEMPLATES_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(TRAIN_TYPE_VARIANTS_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(DATA_STANDARDS_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(STANDARD_7_7_2_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    _copy_if_missing(BUNDLED_TRAIN_TYPE_VALUES_FILE, TRAIN_TYPE_VALUES_FILE)
    _copy_if_missing(BUNDLED_EXCEL_MAPPING_FILE, EXCEL_MAPPING_FILE)
    _copy_if_missing(BUNDLED_METADATA_FILE, METADATA_FILE)
    _copy_if_missing(BUNDLED_TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE, TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE)
    _copy_if_missing(BUNDLED_GENERIC_VARIANT_FILE, GENERIC_VARIANT_FILE)
    _copy_if_missing(BUNDLED_LIGHT_DIESEL_VARIANT_FILE, LIGHT_DIESEL_VARIANT_FILE)
    _copy_if_missing(BUNDLED_HEAVY_DIESEL_VARIANT_FILE, HEAVY_DIESEL_VARIANT_FILE)
    _copy_if_missing(BUNDLED_STANDARD_7_7_2_METADATA_FILE, STANDARD_7_7_2_METADATA_FILE)
    _copy_if_missing(BUNDLED_STANDARD_7_7_2_WORKBOOK, STANDARD_7_7_2_WORKBOOK)

from __future__ import annotations

import queue
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from app.comparison.models import ComparisonError, StandardMetadataError, WorkbookComparisonResult, WorkbookLoadError
from app.comparison.standard_metadata import StandardWorkbookMetadata
from app.comparison.workbook_comparator import WorkbookComparator


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ComparisonWorkerProgress:
    message: str
    kind: str = field(init=False, default="progress")


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ComparisonWorkerSuccess:
    result: WorkbookComparisonResult
    kind: str = field(init=False, default="success")


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ComparisonWorkerFailure:
    exception: Exception
    error_kind: str
    kind: str = field(init=False, default="error")


ComparisonWorkerMessage = ComparisonWorkerProgress | ComparisonWorkerSuccess | ComparisonWorkerFailure


def run_comparison_worker(
    project_workbook_path: str,
    metadata: StandardWorkbookMetadata,
    message_queue: queue.Queue[ComparisonWorkerMessage],
    comparator_factory: type[WorkbookComparator] = WorkbookComparator,
) -> None:
    comparator: WorkbookComparator | None = None

    def progress_callback(message: str) -> None:
        message_queue.put(ComparisonWorkerProgress(message=message))

    try:
        comparator = comparator_factory(
            project_workbook_path=project_workbook_path,
            metadata=metadata,
            progress_callback=progress_callback,
        )
        result = comparator.compare_sheets()
        message_queue.put(ComparisonWorkerSuccess(result=result))
    except StandardMetadataError as exc:
        message_queue.put(ComparisonWorkerFailure(exception=exc, error_kind="standard_metadata"))
    except WorkbookLoadError as exc:
        message_queue.put(ComparisonWorkerFailure(exception=exc, error_kind="workbook_load"))
    except ComparisonError as exc:
        message_queue.put(ComparisonWorkerFailure(exception=exc, error_kind="comparison"))
    except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - unexpected fallback
        message_queue.put(ComparisonWorkerFailure(exception=exc, error_kind="unexpected"))
    finally:
        if comparator is not None:
            comparator.close_workbooks()

from typing import Any

from app.comparison.models import DeviationRecord


def is_placeholder(value: Any) -> bool:
    if value is None:
        return True
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value.strip() in {"", "-"}
    return False


def is_actionable(deviation: DeviationRecord) -> bool:
    if deviation.parameter.strip() or deviation.rule.strip():
        return True
    return not is_placeholder(deviation.standard_value)

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any


class ComparisonError(Exception):
    """Base exception for workbook comparison failures."""


class WorkbookLoadError(ComparisonError):
    """Raised when one or both workbooks cannot be loaded."""


class ReportGenerationError(ComparisonError):
    """Raised when the deviation report cannot be written."""


class StandardMetadataError(ComparisonError):
    """Raised when the embedded standard metadata cannot be loaded or validated."""


@dataclass(slots=True)
class DeviationRecord:
    change_type: str
    sheet_name: str
    cell_address: str
    standard_value: Any
    project_value: Any
    parameter: str = ""
    rule: str = ""


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ComparisonSummary:
    total_sheets_compared: int = 0
    total_cells_compared: int = 0
    total_deviations_found: int = 0
    execution_time_seconds: float = 0.0


@dataclass(slots=True)
class WorkbookComparisonResult:
    deviations: list[DeviationRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
    summary: ComparisonSummary = field(default_factory=ComparisonSummary)
    output_file: str = ""
    standard_display_name: str = ""
    standard_workbook_file: str = ""
    project_workbook_file: str = ""


@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)
class PassiveRuleMatch:

    rule: str

FILE: app\comparison\passive_rules_parser.py | SIZE: 9.37 KB | ENCODING: utf-8-sig
========================================================================================================================

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any
from bisect import bisect_right

from openpyxl.utils.cell import get_column_letter, range_boundaries

from app.comparison.models import PassiveRuleMatch
from app.comparison.standard_metadata import StandardWorkbookMetadata

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass(slots=True)
class _IndexedCell:
    row: int
    column: int
    coordinate: str
    value: str


@dataclass(slots=True)
class _SheetIndex:
    entries: list[_IndexedCell] = field(default_factory=list)
    value_positions: dict[str, list[int]] = field(default_factory=dict)


class PassiveRulesParser:
    def __init__(self, metadata: StandardWorkbookMetadata) -> None:
        self._metadata = metadata
        self._sheet_indexes: dict[str, _SheetIndex] = {}

    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_text(value: Any) -> str | None:
        if value is None:
            return None
        if isinstance(value, str):
            normalized = value.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n").strip()
            return normalized or None
        return str(value).strip() or None

    @staticmethod
    def _safe_int(value: Any, default: int | None = None) -> int | None:
        try:
            return int(value)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return default

    @staticmethod
    def _sheet_bounds(worksheet):
        try:
            dimension = worksheet.calculate_dimension()
        except ValueError:
            return None

        if dimension == "A1:A1":
            first_value = worksheet.iter_rows(min_row=1, max_row=1, min_col=1, max_col=1, values_only=True)
            first_row = next(first_value, (None,))
            if not first_row or first_row[0] is None:
                return None

        return range_boundaries(dimension)

    def _build_sheet_index(self, workbook, sheet_name: str) -> _SheetIndex:
        cached = self._sheet_indexes.get(sheet_name)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached

        if sheet_name not in workbook.sheetnames:
            self._sheet_indexes[sheet_name] = _SheetIndex()
            return self._sheet_indexes[sheet_name]

        worksheet = workbook[sheet_name]
        bounds = self._sheet_bounds(worksheet)
        if bounds is None:
            self._sheet_indexes[sheet_name] = _SheetIndex()
            return self._sheet_indexes[sheet_name]

        min_col, min_row, max_col, max_row = bounds
        entries: list[_IndexedCell] = []
        value_positions: dict[str, list[int]] = {}

        for row in worksheet.iter_rows(min_row=min_row, max_row=max_row, min_col=min_col, max_col=max_col, values_only=False):
            for cell in row:
                normalized = self._normalize_text(cell.value)
                if normalized is None:
                    continue
                index = len(entries)
                entries.append(
                    _IndexedCell(
                        row=cell.row,
                        column=cell.column,
                        coordinate=cell.coordinate,
                        value=normalized,
                    )
                )
                value_positions.setdefault(normalized, []).append(index)

        indexed_sheet = _SheetIndex(entries=entries, value_positions=value_positions)
        self._sheet_indexes[sheet_name] = indexed_sheet
        return indexed_sheet

    def _find_anchor_index(
        self,
        sheet_index: _SheetIndex,
        parameter: str,
        find_after: str | None = None,
    ) -> int | None:
        parameter_key = self._normalize_text(parameter)
        if parameter_key is None:
            return None

        if find_after:
            anchor_key = self._normalize_text(find_after)
            if anchor_key is None:
                return None
            anchor_positions = sheet_index.value_positions.get(anchor_key, [])
            parameter_positions = sheet_index.value_positions.get(parameter_key, [])
            if not parameter_positions:
                return None
            for anchor_position in anchor_positions:
                candidate_offset = bisect_right(parameter_positions, anchor_position)
                if candidate_offset < len(parameter_positions):
                    return parameter_positions[candidate_offset]
            return None

        positions = sheet_index.value_positions.get(parameter_key, [])
        return positions[0] if positions else None

    def build_map(self, workbook) -> dict[tuple[str, str], list[PassiveRuleMatch]]:
        self._sheet_indexes.clear()

        if not self._metadata.passive_rules_enabled:
            return {}

        passive_rules_sheet_name = self._metadata.passive_rules_sheet_name
        if passive_rules_sheet_name not in workbook.sheetnames:
            logger.warning("Passive rules sheet '%s' not found.", passive_rules_sheet_name)
            return {}

        passive_rules_sheet = workbook[passive_rules_sheet_name]
        columns = self._metadata.passive_rules_columns
        data_start_row = self._metadata.passive_rules_data_start_row
        max_row = passive_rules_sheet.max_row or data_start_row
        current_rule = ""
        matches_by_cell: dict[tuple[str, str], list[PassiveRuleMatch]] = {}
        parsed_candidate_rows = 0
        mapped_rows = 0
        missing_sheet_rows = 0
        invalid_offset_rows = 0
        missing_anchor_rows = 0
        invalid_target_rows = 0
        unexpected_error_rows = 0

        for row_number in range(data_start_row, max_row + 1):
            try:
                parsed_candidate_rows += 1
                rule_value = passive_rules_sheet[f"{columns.get('rule', 'A')}{row_number}"].value
                if rule_value not in (None, ""):
                    current_rule = self._normalize_text(rule_value) or current_rule

                parameter = self._normalize_text(passive_rules_sheet[f"{columns.get('parameter', 'C')}{row_number}"].value)
                sheet_name = self._normalize_text(passive_rules_sheet[f"{columns.get('sheet', 'E')}{row_number}"].value)
                row_offset = self._safe_int(passive_rules_sheet[f"{columns.get('row_offset', 'F')}{row_number}"].value)
                column_offset = self._safe_int(passive_rules_sheet[f"{columns.get('column_offset', 'G')}{row_number}"].value)
                find_after = self._normalize_text(passive_rules_sheet[f"{columns.get('find_after', 'K')}{row_number}"].value)
                display_name = self._normalize_text(passive_rules_sheet[f"{columns.get('display_name', 'L')}{row_number}"].value)

                if not parameter or not sheet_name:
                    continue
                if row_offset is None or column_offset is None:
                    invalid_offset_rows += 1
                    logger.debug("Skipping passive rule row %s due to invalid offsets.", row_number)
                    continue
                if sheet_name not in workbook.sheetnames:
                    missing_sheet_rows += 1
                    logger.debug("Passive rule row %s references missing sheet '%s'.", row_number, sheet_name)
                    continue

                sheet_index = self._build_sheet_index(workbook, sheet_name)
                anchor_index = self._find_anchor_index(sheet_index, parameter, find_after=find_after)
                if anchor_index is None:
                    missing_anchor_rows += 1
                    logger.debug(
                        "Passive rule row %s could not locate parameter '%s' on sheet '%s'.",
                        row_number,
                        parameter,
                        sheet_name,
                    )
                    continue

                anchor_cell = sheet_index.entries[anchor_index]
                target_row = anchor_cell.row + row_offset
                target_column = anchor_cell.column + column_offset
                if target_row < 1 or target_column < 1:
                    invalid_target_rows += 1
                    logger.debug("Skipping passive rule row %s due to invalid target cell offsets.", row_number)
                    continue

                target_address = f"{get_column_letter(target_column)}{target_row}"
                report_parameter = display_name or parameter
                report_rule = current_rule or ""
                cell_key = (sheet_name, target_address)
                candidate = PassiveRuleMatch(parameter=report_parameter, rule=report_rule)
                matches = matches_by_cell.setdefault(cell_key, [])
                if candidate not in matches:
                    matches.append(candidate)
                    mapped_rows += 1
            except Exception:
                unexpected_error_rows += 1
                logger.warning("Unexpected passive rule parsing error on row %s.", row_number, exc_info=True)
                continue

        logger.info(
            "Passive rules parsed: parsed_candidate_rows=%s mapped_rows=%s missing_sheet_rows=%s invalid_offset_rows=%s missing_anchor_rows=%s invalid_target_rows=%s unexpected_error_rows=%s",
            parsed_candidate_rows,
            mapped_rows,
            missing_sheet_rows,
            invalid_offset_rows,
            missing_anchor_rows,
            invalid_target_rows,
            unexpected_error_rows,
        )

        return matches_by_cell

import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ReportGenerator:
    def __init__(self, metadata: StandardWorkbookMetadata | None = None) -> None:
        self._metadata = metadata

    @property
    def report_sheet_name(self) -> str:
        if self._metadata is not None:
            return self._metadata.report_sheet_name
        return "List of Project Deviations"

    @property
    def headers(self) -> list[str]:
        return [
            "Change No",
            "Sheet",
            "Cell",
            "Standard Value",
            "Project Value",
            "Parameter",
            "Rule",
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _sheet_sort_key(item: Any) -> tuple[str, int, int]:
        sheet_name = item.sheet_name or ""
        cell = item.cell_address or ""
        if cell == "-":
            return sheet_name, -1, -1
        try:
            from openpyxl.utils.cell import coordinate_from_string, column_index_from_string

            column, row = coordinate_from_string(cell)
            return sheet_name, row, column_index_from_string(column)
        except Exception:
            return sheet_name, 10**9, 10**9

    @staticmethod
    def _display_value(value: Any) -> Any:
        return "" if value is None else value

    @staticmethod
    def _count_by_sheet(deviations: list[Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
        counts = defaultdict(lambda: {"raw": 0, "actionable": 0, "mapped": 0})
        for deviation in deviations:
            sheet_name = deviation.sheet_name or ""
            counts[sheet_name]["raw"] += 1
            if is_actionable(deviation):
                counts[sheet_name]["actionable"] += 1
            if deviation.parameter.strip() or deviation.rule.strip():
                counts[sheet_name]["mapped"] += 1
        return counts

    @staticmethod
    def _apply_range_style(worksheet: Any, cell_range: str, fill, font, alignment, border=None) -> None:
        cell = worksheet[cell_range.split(":")[0]]
        cell.fill = fill
        cell.font = copy(font)
        cell.alignment = copy(alignment)
        if border is not None:
            cell.border = copy(border)

    @staticmethod
    def _configure_summary_sheet(workbook: Workbook, result: WorkbookComparisonResult):
        worksheet = workbook.create_sheet(SUMMARY_SHEET_NAME)
        worksheet.sheet_view.showGridLines = False
        worksheet.sheet_view.zoomScale = 90
        worksheet.freeze_panes = "A8"
        worksheet.sheet_properties.tabColor = SUMMARY_TAB_COLOR
        worksheet.page_setup.orientation = "landscape"
        worksheet.page_setup.fitToWidth = 1
        worksheet.page_setup.fitToHeight = 0
        worksheet.page_margins = SUMMARY_PAGE_MARGINS
        worksheet.print_title_rows = "1:7"

        for column, width in SUMMARY_COLUMN_WIDTHS.items():
            worksheet.column_dimensions[column].width = width

        worksheet.row_dimensions[2].height = 30
        worksheet.row_dimensions[3].height = 22
        worksheet.row_dimensions[5].height = 18
        worksheet.row_dimensions[6].height = 18
        worksheet.row_dimensions[7].height = 18
        worksheet.row_dimensions[9].height = 18
        worksheet.row_dimensions[10].height = 24
        worksheet.row_dimensions[11].height = 24
        worksheet.row_dimensions[13].height = 20
        worksheet.row_dimensions[14].height = 18
        worksheet.row_dimensions[15].height = 18

        worksheet["B2"] = "FastPrep Comparison Report"
        worksheet.merge_cells("B2:F2")
        ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
            worksheet,
            "B2:F2",
            SUMMARY_TITLE_FILL,
            SUMMARY_TITLE_FONT,
            SUMMARY_TITLE_ALIGNMENT,
        )

        worksheet["B3"] = "Project deviations against embedded standard"
        worksheet.merge_cells("B3:F3")
        ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
            worksheet,
            "B3:F3",
            SUMMARY_SUBTITLE_FILL,
            SUMMARY_SUBTITLE_FONT,
            SUMMARY_TITLE_ALIGNMENT,
        )

        standard_display_name = result.standard_display_name or ""
        standard_file = result.standard_workbook_file or ""
        project_file = result.project_workbook_file or ""

        metadata_rows = [
            (5, "Standard", standard_display_name),
            (6, "Standard file", standard_file),
            (7, "Project file", project_file),
        ]
        for row_number, label, value in metadata_rows:
            worksheet[f"B{row_number}"] = label
            worksheet[f"C{row_number}"] = value
            worksheet.merge_cells(f"C{row_number}:F{row_number}")
            ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
                worksheet,
                f"B{row_number}:B{row_number}",
                SUMMARY_LABEL_FILL,
                SUMMARY_LABEL_FONT,
                SUMMARY_LEFT_ALIGNMENT,
                SUMMARY_VALUE_BORDER,
            )
            ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
                worksheet,
                f"C{row_number}:F{row_number}",
                SUMMARY_VALUE_FILL,
                SUMMARY_VALUE_FONT,
                SUMMARY_LEFT_ALIGNMENT,
                SUMMARY_VALUE_BORDER,
            )

        raw_deviations = list(result.deviations)
        actionable_deviations = [deviation for deviation in raw_deviations if is_actionable(deviation)]
        mapped_count = sum(1 for deviation in raw_deviations if deviation.parameter.strip() or deviation.rule.strip())

        card_specs = [
            ("B9:C9", "B10:C11", "Raw differences", len(raw_deviations)),
            ("D9:E9", "D10:E11", "Actionable differences", len(actionable_deviations)),
            ("F9:G9", "F10:G11", "Passive Rules mapped", mapped_count),
        ]
        for label_range, value_range, label, value in card_specs:
            start_label = label_range.split(":")[0]
            start_value = value_range.split(":")[0]
            worksheet[start_label] = label
            worksheet[start_value] = value
            worksheet[start_value].number_format = "#,##0"
            worksheet.merge_cells(label_range)
            worksheet.merge_cells(value_range)
            ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
                worksheet,
                label_range,
                SUMMARY_KPI_LABEL_FILL,
                SUMMARY_LABEL_FONT,
                SUMMARY_CENTER_ALIGNMENT,
                SUMMARY_KPI_BORDER,
            )
            ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
                worksheet,
                value_range,
                SUMMARY_KPI_VALUE_FILL,
                SUMMARY_KPI_VALUE_FONT,
                SUMMARY_CENTER_ALIGNMENT,
                SUMMARY_KPI_BORDER,
            )

        metric_specs = [
            ("B13", "Sheets compared", result.summary.total_sheets_compared, "#,##0"),
            ("D13", "Non-empty positions compared", result.summary.total_cells_compared, "#,##0"),
            ("F13", "Execution time [s]", result.summary.execution_time_seconds, "0.00"),
        ]
        for label_cell, label, value, number_format in metric_specs:
            value_cell = chr(ord(label_cell[0]) + 1) + label_cell[1:]
            worksheet[label_cell] = label
            worksheet[value_cell] = value
            worksheet[label_cell].fill = copy(SUMMARY_METRIC_LABEL_FILL)
            worksheet[label_cell].font = copy(SUMMARY_METRIC_FONT)
            worksheet[label_cell].alignment = SUMMARY_LEFT_ALIGNMENT
            worksheet[value_cell].fill = SUMMARY_METRIC_VALUE_FILL
            worksheet[value_cell].font = copy(SUMMARY_METRIC_FONT)
            worksheet[value_cell].alignment = SUMMARY_CENTER_ALIGNMENT
            worksheet[label_cell].border = copy(SUMMARY_VALUE_BORDER)
            worksheet[value_cell].border = copy(SUMMARY_VALUE_BORDER)
            worksheet[value_cell].number_format = number_format

        legend_items = [
            ("B14:C14", "green = Passive Rules mapping found", SUMMARY_POSITIVE_FILL),
            ("D14:E14", "yellow = existing standard value changed", SUMMARY_WARNING_FILL),
            ("F14:G14", "blue = value populated where standard was blank/-", SUMMARY_INFO_FILL),
            ("B15:G15", "red = missing project value or missing sheet", SUMMARY_ALERT_FILL),
        ]
        for cell_range, text, fill in legend_items:
            start_cell = cell_range.split(":")[0]
            worksheet[start_cell] = text
            worksheet.merge_cells(cell_range)
            ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
                worksheet,
                cell_range,
                fill,
                SUMMARY_LEGEND_FONT,
                SUMMARY_LEFT_ALIGNMENT,
            )
        worksheet["B15"].font = copy(SUMMARY_NOTE_FONT)

        sheet_counts = ReportGenerator._count_by_sheet(raw_deviations)
        summary_rows = [
            (sheet_name, counts["raw"], counts["actionable"], counts["mapped"])
            for sheet_name, counts in sheet_counts.items()
        ]
        summary_rows.sort(key=lambda row: (-row[1], row[0]))

        worksheet["B16"] = "Sheet"
        worksheet["C16"] = "Raw differences"
        worksheet["D16"] = "Actionable differences"
        worksheet["E16"] = "Passive Rules mapped"

        summary_data_end_row = 16 + max(len(summary_rows), 1)
        if summary_rows:
            for index, row in enumerate(summary_rows, start=17):
                worksheet[f"B{index}"] = row[0]
                worksheet[f"C{index}"] = row[1]
                worksheet[f"D{index}"] = row[2]
                worksheet[f"E{index}"] = row[3]
        else:
            worksheet["B17"] = ""
            worksheet["C17"] = 0
            worksheet["D17"] = 0
            worksheet["E17"] = 0

        summary_table = Table(displayName=SUMMARY_TABLE_NAME, ref=f"B16:E{summary_data_end_row}")
        summary_table.tableStyleInfo = TableStyleInfo(
            name="TableStyleMedium2",
            showFirstColumn=False,
            showLastColumn=False,
            showRowStripes=True,
            showColumnStripes=False,
        )
        worksheet.add_table(summary_table)

        for row in range(17, summary_data_end_row + 1):
            worksheet[f"C{row}"].number_format = "#,##0"
            worksheet[f"D{row}"].number_format = "#,##0"
            worksheet[f"E{row}"].number_format = "#,##0"

        if summary_rows:
            worksheet.conditional_formatting.add(
                f"C17:C{summary_data_end_row}",
                DataBarRule(start_type="min", start_value=None, end_type="max", end_value=None, color="5B9BD5"),
            )
            worksheet.conditional_formatting.add(
                f"D17:D{summary_data_end_row}",
                DataBarRule(start_type="min", start_value=None, end_type="max", end_value=None, color="5B9BD5"),
            )

        footer_row = summary_data_end_row + 2
        worksheet.merge_cells(start_row=footer_row, start_column=2, end_row=footer_row, end_column=5)
        footer_cell = worksheet.cell(footer_row, 2)
        footer_cell.value = (
            "All Cell Differences contains the complete unfiltered comparison output. "
            "List of Project Deviations is the actionable subset."
        )
        footer_cell.fill = SUMMARY_NOTE_FILL
        footer_cell.font = copy(SUMMARY_NOTE_FONT)
        footer_cell.alignment = SUMMARY_LEFT_ALIGNMENT
        worksheet.row_dimensions[footer_row].height = 32

        return worksheet

    @staticmethod
    def _configure_deviation_sheet(
        worksheet,
        tab_color: str,
        subtitle_text: str,
        count_text: str,
    ) -> None:
        worksheet.sheet_view.showGridLines = False
        worksheet.sheet_view.zoomScale = 85
        worksheet.freeze_panes = "A5"
        worksheet.sheet_properties.tabColor = tab_color
        worksheet.page_setup.orientation = "landscape"
        worksheet.page_setup.fitToWidth = 1
        worksheet.page_setup.fitToHeight = 0
        worksheet.page_margins = DEVIATION_PAGE_MARGINS
        worksheet.print_title_rows = "1:4"

        for column, width in DEVIATION_COLUMN_WIDTHS.items():
            worksheet.column_dimensions[column].width = width

        worksheet.row_dimensions[1].height = 28
        worksheet.row_dimensions[2].height = 24
        worksheet.row_dimensions[3].height = 20
        worksheet.row_dimensions[4].height = 28

        worksheet["A1"] = worksheet.title
        worksheet.merge_cells("A1:G1")
        ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
            worksheet,
            "A1:G1",
            SUMMARY_TITLE_FILL,
            DEVIATION_TITLE_FONT,
            SUMMARY_TITLE_ALIGNMENT,
        )

        worksheet["A2"] = subtitle_text
        worksheet.merge_cells("A2:G2")
        ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
            worksheet,
            "A2:G2",
            SUMMARY_SUBTITLE_FILL,
            DEVIATION_SUBTITLE_FONT,
            SUMMARY_LEFT_ALIGNMENT,
        )

        worksheet["A3"] = count_text
        worksheet.merge_cells("A3:G3")
        ReportGenerator._apply_range_style(
            worksheet,
            "A3:G3",
            SUMMARY_NOTE_FILL,
            DEVIATION_COUNT_FONT,
            SUMMARY_TITLE_ALIGNMENT,
        )

        headers = [
            "Change No",
            "Sheet",
            "Cell",
            "Standard Value",
            "Project Value",
            "Parameter",
            "Rule",
        ]
        for index, header in enumerate(headers, start=1):
            cell = worksheet.cell(4, index)
            cell.value = header
            cell.fill = DEVIATION_HEADER_FILL
            cell.font = copy(DEVIATION_HEADER_FONT)
            cell.alignment = DEVIATION_HEADER_ALIGNMENT
            cell.border = DEVIATION_HEADER_BORDER

    @staticmethod
    def _style_deviation_row(worksheet, row_number: int, deviation) -> None:
        standard_is_placeholder = is_placeholder(deviation.standard_value)
        project_is_placeholder = is_placeholder(deviation.project_value)
        is_missing_sheet = deviation.cell_address == "-"
        is_mapped = bool(deviation.parameter.strip() or deviation.rule.strip())
        both_non_placeholder = not standard_is_placeholder and not project_is_placeholder

        for column in range(1, 8):
            cell = worksheet.cell(row_number, column)
            cell.border = copy(DEVIATION_BASE_BORDER)
            cell.font = copy(DEVIATION_BASE_FONT)
            cell.alignment = copy(DEVIATION_COLUMN_ALIGNMENTS[column])

        if is_missing_sheet:
            for column in range(1, 8):
                cell = worksheet.cell(row_number, column)
                cell.fill = DEVIATION_MISSING_SHEET_FILL
                cell.font = copy(DEVIATION_MISSING_FONT)
            return

        if is_mapped:
            for column in (6, 7):
                cell = worksheet.cell(row_number, column)
                cell.fill = DEVIATION_MAPPED_FILL
                cell.font = copy(DEVIATION_MAPPED_FONT)

        if both_non_placeholder:
            for column in (4, 5):
                worksheet.cell(row_number, column).fill = DEVIATION_YELLOW_FILL
        elif standard_is_placeholder and not project_is_placeholder:
            worksheet.cell(row_number, 5).fill = DEVIATION_BLUE_FILL
        elif project_is_placeholder and not standard_is_placeholder:
            worksheet.cell(row_number, 4).fill = DEVIATION_RED_FILL

    def _write_deviation_sheet(
        self,
        workbook: Workbook,
        title: str,
        subtitle_text: str,
        tab_color: str,
        table_name: str,
        table_style_name: str,
        deviations,
        actionable_only: bool,
    ):
        worksheet = workbook.create_sheet(title)
        rows = list(deviations)
        visible_rows = [row for row in rows if is_actionable(row)] if actionable_only else rows
        count_text = f"Rows: {len(visible_rows)} of {len(rows)} raw differences" if actionable_only else f"Rows: {len(visible_rows)}"
        self._configure_deviation_sheet(worksheet, tab_color, subtitle_text, count_text)

        sorted_rows = sorted(visible_rows, key=self._sheet_sort_key)

        if not sorted_rows:
            worksheet.append([None] * 7)
        else:
            for change_no, deviation in enumerate(sorted_rows, start=1):
                worksheet.append(
                    [
                        change_no,
                        deviation.sheet_name,
                        deviation.cell_address,
                        self._display_value(deviation.standard_value),
                        self._display_value(deviation.project_value),
                        self._display_value(deviation.parameter),
                        self._display_value(deviation.rule),
                    ]
                )

        last_row = worksheet.max_row
        for row_number, deviation in enumerate(sorted_rows, start=5):
            self._style_deviation_row(worksheet, row_number, deviation)

        table_ref_end = last_row if visible_rows else 5
        table = Table(displayName=table_name, ref=f"A4:G{table_ref_end}")
        table.tableStyleInfo = TableStyleInfo(
            name=table_style_name,
            showFirstColumn=False,
            showLastColumn=False,
            showRowStripes=True,
            showColumnStripes=False,
        )
        worksheet.add_table(table)

        return worksheet

    def generate(self, result: WorkbookComparisonResult, output_path: str) -> str:
        try:
            workbook = Workbook()
            workbook.remove(workbook.active)
            workbook.properties.title = "FastPrep Comparison Report"
            workbook.properties.subject = "Project deviations against embedded standard"
            workbook.properties.creator = "Train Type Data Preparation Tool"

            _summary_sheet = self._configure_summary_sheet(workbook, result)
            _actionable_sheet = self._write_deviation_sheet(
                workbook,
                ACTIONABLE_SHEET_NAME,
                "Actionable differences. Rows mapped by Passive Rules are highlighted.",
                ACTIONABLE_TAB_COLOR,
                ACTIONABLE_TABLE_NAME,
                "TableStyleMedium2",
                result.deviations,
                actionable_only=True,
            )
            _raw_sheet = self._write_deviation_sheet(
                workbook,
                RAW_SHEET_NAME,
                "Complete unfiltered cell-by-cell comparison output.",
                RAW_TAB_COLOR,
                RAW_TABLE_NAME,
                "TableStyleMedium4",
                result.deviations,
                actionable_only=False,
            )

            output_file = Path(output_path)
            output_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            workbook.save(output_file)
            logger.info("Deviation report saved to: %s", output_file)
            return str(output_file)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Failed to generate deviation report.")
            raise ReportGenerationError(f"Failed to generate deviation report: {exc}") from exc

from openpyxl.styles import Alignment, Border, Font, PatternFill, Side
from openpyxl.worksheet.page import PageMargins

SUMMARY_SHEET_NAME = "Summary"
ACTIONABLE_SHEET_NAME = "List of Project Deviations"
RAW_SHEET_NAME = "All Cell Differences"

SUMMARY_TABLE_NAME = "SummaryBySheet"
ACTIONABLE_TABLE_NAME = "ActionableDeviationsTable"
RAW_TABLE_NAME = "AllCellDifferencesTable"

SUMMARY_TAB_COLOR = "1F4E78"
ACTIONABLE_TAB_COLOR = "70AD47"
RAW_TAB_COLOR = "A5A5A5"

SUMMARY_COLUMN_WIDTHS = {
    "A": 3,
    "B": 32,
    "C": 22,
    "D": 22,
    "E": 22,
    "F": 22,
    "G": 12,
}

DEVIATION_COLUMN_WIDTHS = {
    "A": 12,
    "B": 32,
    "C": 12,
    "D": 34,
    "E": 34,
    "F": 44,
    "G": 42,
}

SUMMARY_PAGE_MARGINS = PageMargins(left=0.25, right=0.25, top=0.5, bottom=0.5, header=0.2, footer=0.2)

FILE: app\comparison\standard_metadata.py | SIZE: 4.52 KB | ENCODING: utf-8-sig

import json
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from app.comparison.models import StandardMetadataError


@dataclass(slots=True)
class StandardWorkbookMetadata:
    standard_id: str
    display_name: str
    baseline: str
    train_type: str
    workbook_file: str
    read_only: bool
    precalculated: bool
    password: str
    compare: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    passive_rules: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    report_columns: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    user_warning: str = ""
    source_path: str = ""

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, metadata_path: str) -> "StandardWorkbookMetadata":
        resolved_metadata_path = os.path.abspath(os.fspath(metadata_path))
        if not os.path.exists(resolved_metadata_path):
            raise StandardMetadataError(f"Standard metadata file not found: {resolved_metadata_path}")

        try:
            with open(resolved_metadata_path, encoding="utf-8-sig") as file_obj:
                raw = json.load(file_obj)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise StandardMetadataError(
                f"Invalid JSON in standard metadata file {resolved_metadata_path} at line {exc.lineno}, column {exc.colno}: {exc.msg}"
            ) from exc
        except OSError as exc:
            raise StandardMetadataError(f"Unable to read standard metadata file {resolved_metadata_path}: {exc}") from exc

        required_fields = ["standard_id", "display_name", "baseline", "train_type", "workbook_file"]
        missing_fields = [field_name for field_name in required_fields if not raw.get(field_name)]
        if missing_fields:
            raise StandardMetadataError(
                f"Standard metadata file {resolved_metadata_path} is missing required fields: {', '.join(missing_fields)}"
            )

        workbook_file = raw["workbook_file"]
        if os.path.isabs(workbook_file):
            workbook_path = workbook_file
        else:
            workbook_path = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(resolved_metadata_path), workbook_file))

        if not os.path.exists(workbook_path):
            raise StandardMetadataError(
                f"Standard workbook not found: {workbook_path} (referenced by metadata {resolved_metadata_path})"
            )

        return cls(
            standard_id=raw["standard_id"],
            display_name=raw["display_name"],
            baseline=raw["baseline"],
            train_type=raw["train_type"],
            workbook_file=workbook_file,
            read_only=bool(raw.get("read_only", True)),
            precalculated=bool(raw.get("precalculated", True)),
            password=raw.get("password", ""),
            compare=dict(raw.get("compare", {})),
            passive_rules=dict(raw.get("passive_rules", {})),
            report_columns=list(raw.get("report_columns", [])),
            user_warning=raw.get("user_warning", ""),
            source_path=resolved_metadata_path,
        )

    @property
    def base_dir(self) -> str:
        return os.path.dirname(self.source_path) if self.source_path else ""

    @property
    def workbook_path(self) -> str:
        if os.path.isabs(self.workbook_file):
            return self.workbook_file
        return os.path.abspath(os.path.join(self.base_dir, self.workbook_file))

    @property
    def report_name(self) -> str:
        return self.compare.get("report_name", "Workbook_Deviations.xlsx")

    @property
    def report_sheet_name(self) -> str:
        return self.compare.get("report_sheet_name", "List of Project Deviations")

    @property
    def ignored_sheets(self) -> set[str]:
        return set(self.compare.get("ignored_sheets", []))

    @property
    def ignored_prefixes(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        return tuple(self.compare.get("ignored_prefixes", []))

    @property
    def passive_rules_enabled(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.passive_rules.get("enabled", False))

    @property
    def passive_rules_sheet_name(self) -> str:
        return self.passive_rules.get("sheet_name", "Passive Rules Check")

    @property
    def passive_rules_header_row(self) -> int:
        return int(self.passive_rules.get("header_row", 182))

    @property
    def passive_rules_data_start_row(self) -> int:
        return int(self.passive_rules.get("data_start_row", 183))

    @property
    def passive_rules_columns(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        return dict(self.passive_rules.get("columns", {}))

========================================================================================================================
FILE: app\comparison\workbook_comparator.py | SIZE: 13.77 KB | ENCODING: utf-8-sig
========================================================================================================================

import io
import logging
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

import openpyxl
from openpyxl.utils.cell import get_column_letter, range_boundaries

from app.comparison.models import (
    ComparisonSummary,
    DeviationRecord,
    PassiveRuleMatch,
    WorkbookComparisonResult,
    WorkbookLoadError,
)
from app.comparison.passive_rules_parser import PassiveRulesParser
from app.comparison.report_generator import ReportGenerator
from app.comparison.standard_metadata import StandardWorkbookMetadata
from app.config import STANDARD_7_7_2_METADATA_FILE

MAX_COMPARISON_AREA = 10_000_000

try:
    import msoffcrypto
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover - optional dependency
    msoffcrypto = None

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

ProgressCallback = Callable[[str], None]


class WorkbookComparator:
    def __init__(
        self,
        project_workbook_path: str,
        standard_metadata_path: str = STANDARD_7_7_2_METADATA_FILE,
        password: str | None = None,
        report_generator: ReportGenerator | None = None,
        progress_callback: ProgressCallback | None = None,
        metadata: StandardWorkbookMetadata | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._project_workbook_path = project_workbook_path
        self._metadata = metadata or StandardWorkbookMetadata.load(standard_metadata_path)
        self._standard_workbook_path = self._metadata.workbook_path
        self._password = password or self._metadata.password
        self._report_generator = report_generator or ReportGenerator(self._metadata)
        self._progress_callback = progress_callback
        self._standard_workbook = None
        self._project_workbook = None
        self._passive_match_map: dict[tuple[str, str], list[PassiveRuleMatch]] = {}
        self._standard_display_name = self._metadata.display_name
        self._standard_workbook_file = Path(self._standard_workbook_path).name
        self._project_workbook_file = Path(self._project_workbook_path).name

    @property
    def standard_display_name(self) -> str:
        return self._metadata.display_name

    @property
    def user_warning(self) -> str:
        return self._metadata.user_warning

    @property
    def report_name(self) -> str:
        return self._metadata.report_name

    def _progress(self, message: str) -> None:
        logger.info(message)
        if self._progress_callback is not None:
            self._progress_callback(message)

    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_value(value):
        if value is None:
            return None
        if isinstance(value, str):
            normalized = value.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n").strip()
            return normalized if normalized else None
        return value

    @staticmethod
    def _sheet_bounds(worksheet: Any) -> tuple[int, int, int, int] | None:
        try:
            dimension = worksheet.calculate_dimension()
        except ValueError:
            return None

        if dimension == "A1:A1":
            first_value = worksheet.iter_rows(min_row=1, max_row=1, min_col=1, max_col=1, values_only=True)
            first_row = next(first_value, (None,))
            if not first_row or first_row[0] is None:
                return None

        return range_boundaries(dimension)

    def _load_protected_workbook(self, file_path: str) -> Any:
        if msoffcrypto is None:
            raise WorkbookLoadError(
                "This workbook appears to be password-protected, but the optional decryption library is not available."
            )

        logger.info("Attempting to decrypt protected workbook: %s", file_path)
        with open(file_path, "rb") as source_file:
            office_file = msoffcrypto.OfficeFile(source_file)
            office_file.load_key(password=self._password)
            decrypted_stream = io.BytesIO()
            office_file.decrypt(decrypted_stream)
            decrypted_stream.seek(0)
            return openpyxl.load_workbook(
                decrypted_stream,
                data_only=True,
                read_only=True,
                keep_vba=True,
            )

    def _load_workbook(self, file_path: str) -> Any:
        resolved_file_path = os.path.abspath(file_path)
        if not os.path.exists(resolved_file_path):
            raise WorkbookLoadError(f"Workbook not found: {resolved_file_path}")

        try:
            return openpyxl.load_workbook(
                filename=resolved_file_path,
                data_only=True,
                read_only=True,
                keep_vba=True,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("openpyxl load failed for %s: %s", resolved_file_path, exc)
            if msoffcrypto is None:
                raise WorkbookLoadError(
                    f"Unable to load workbook '{resolved_file_path}': normal load failed ({exc}) and encrypted fallback is unavailable."
                ) from exc
            try:
                return self._load_protected_workbook(resolved_file_path)
            except Exception as protected_exc:
                raise WorkbookLoadError(
                    f"Unable to load workbook '{resolved_file_path}': normal load failed ({exc}); encrypted fallback failed ({protected_exc})."
                ) from protected_exc

    def load_workbooks(self) -> tuple[Any, Any]:
        self._progress(f"Loading standard workbook: {self._metadata.display_name}")
        self._standard_workbook = self._load_workbook(self._standard_workbook_path)
        self._progress("Loading project workbook...")
        self._project_workbook = self._load_workbook(self._project_workbook_path)

        self._passive_match_map = {}
        if self._metadata.passive_rules_enabled:
            self._progress("Building passive rules map...")
            parser = PassiveRulesParser(self._metadata)
            self._passive_match_map = parser.build_map(self._standard_workbook)
        return self._standard_workbook, self._project_workbook

    def close_workbooks(self) -> None:
        for workbook in (self._standard_workbook, self._project_workbook):
            if workbook is not None:
                try:
                    workbook.close()
                except Exception:
                    logger.debug("Workbook close failed during cleanup.", exc_info=True)
        self._standard_workbook = None
        self._project_workbook = None

    def _visible_compare_sheet_names(self, workbook: Any) -> set[str]:
        ignored_sheets = set(self._metadata.ignored_sheets)
        ignored_prefixes = tuple(self._metadata.ignored_prefixes)
        sheet_names: set[str] = set()
        for sheet_name in workbook.sheetnames:
            worksheet = workbook[sheet_name]
            if worksheet.sheet_state != "visible":
                continue
            if sheet_name in ignored_sheets:
                continue
            if ignored_prefixes and sheet_name.startswith(ignored_prefixes):
                continue
            sheet_names.add(sheet_name)
        return sheet_names

    def _lookup_passive_matches(self, sheet_name: str, cell_address: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
        matches = self._passive_match_map.get((sheet_name, cell_address), [])
        if not matches:
            return "", ""

        parameters = list(dict.fromkeys(match.parameter for match in matches if match.parameter))
        rules = list(dict.fromkeys(match.rule for match in matches if match.rule))
        return "; ".join(parameters), "; ".join(rules)

    def compare_cells(self, standard_sheet: Any, project_sheet: Any) -> tuple[list[DeviationRecord], int]:
        standard_bounds = self._sheet_bounds(standard_sheet)
        project_bounds = self._sheet_bounds(project_sheet)

        if standard_bounds is None and project_bounds is None:
            return [], 0

        if standard_bounds is None:
            min_col, min_row, max_col, max_row = project_bounds
        elif project_bounds is None:
            min_col, min_row, max_col, max_row = standard_bounds
        else:
            min_col = min(standard_bounds[0], project_bounds[0])
            min_row = min(standard_bounds[1], project_bounds[1])
            max_col = max(standard_bounds[2], project_bounds[2])
            max_row = max(standard_bounds[3], project_bounds[3])

        rows = max_row - min_row + 1
        columns = max_col - min_col + 1
        if rows * columns > MAX_COMPARISON_AREA:
            start_cell = f"{get_column_letter(min_col)}{min_row}"
            end_cell = f"{get_column_letter(max_col)}{max_row}"
            raise WorkbookLoadError(
                f"Comparison area too large for sheet '{standard_sheet.title}' ({start_cell}:{end_cell}, {rows} rows x {columns} columns)."
            )

        deviations: list[DeviationRecord] = []
        cells_compared = 0

        standard_rows = standard_sheet.iter_rows(
            min_row=min_row,
            max_row=max_row,
            min_col=min_col,
            max_col=max_col,
            values_only=True,
        )
        project_rows = project_sheet.iter_rows(
            min_row=min_row,
            max_row=max_row,
            min_col=min_col,
            max_col=max_col,
            values_only=True,
        )

        for row_index, (standard_row, project_row) in enumerate(zip(standard_rows, project_rows), start=min_row):
            for col_index, (standard_value, project_value) in enumerate(zip(standard_row, project_row), start=min_col):
                normalized_standard = self._normalize_value(standard_value)
                normalized_project = self._normalize_value(project_value)
                if normalized_standard is None and normalized_project is None:
                    continue

                cells_compared += 1
                if normalized_standard != normalized_project:
                    cell_address = f"{get_column_letter(col_index)}{row_index}"
                    parameter, rule = self._lookup_passive_matches(standard_sheet.title, cell_address)
                    deviations.append(
                        DeviationRecord(
                            change_type="Value Changed",
                            sheet_name=standard_sheet.title,
                            cell_address=cell_address,
                            standard_value=normalized_standard,
                            project_value=normalized_project,
                            parameter=parameter,
                            rule=rule,
                        )
                    )

        return deviations, cells_compared

    def compare_sheets(self) -> WorkbookComparisonResult:
        compare_start = time.perf_counter()
        if self._standard_workbook is None or self._project_workbook is None:
            self.load_workbooks()

        standard_sheets = self._visible_compare_sheet_names(self._standard_workbook)
        project_sheets = self._visible_compare_sheet_names(self._project_workbook)

        common_sheets = sorted(standard_sheets & project_sheets)
        standard_only_sheets = sorted(standard_sheets - project_sheets)
        project_only_sheets = sorted(project_sheets - standard_sheets)

        deviations: list[DeviationRecord] = []
        total_cells_compared = 0

        for index, sheet_name in enumerate(common_sheets, start=1):
            self._progress(f"Comparing sheet {index}/{len(common_sheets)}: {sheet_name}")
            sheet_deviations, cells_compared = self.compare_cells(
                self._standard_workbook[sheet_name],
                self._project_workbook[sheet_name],
            )
            deviations.extend(sheet_deviations)
            total_cells_compared += cells_compared

        for sheet_name in standard_only_sheets:
            deviations.append(
                DeviationRecord(
                    change_type="Missing Sheet",
                    sheet_name=sheet_name,
                    cell_address="-",
                    standard_value="Exists",
                    project_value="Missing",
                    parameter="",
                    rule="",
                )
            )

        for sheet_name in project_only_sheets:
            deviations.append(
                DeviationRecord(
                    change_type="Missing Sheet",
                    sheet_name=sheet_name,
                    cell_address="-",
                    standard_value="Missing",
                    project_value="Exists",
                    parameter="",
                    rule="",
                )
            )

        summary = ComparisonSummary(
            total_sheets_compared=len(common_sheets),
            total_cells_compared=total_cells_compared,
            total_deviations_found=len(deviations),
            execution_time_seconds=round(time.perf_counter() - compare_start, 2),
        )
        return WorkbookComparisonResult(
            deviations=deviations,
            summary=summary,
            standard_display_name=self._standard_display_name,
            standard_workbook_file=self._standard_workbook_file,
            project_workbook_file=self._project_workbook_file,
        )

    def save_report(self, result: WorkbookComparisonResult, output_path: str) -> str:
        return self._report_generator.generate(result, output_path)

    def generate_report(self, output_path: str | None = None) -> WorkbookComparisonResult:
        try:
            self.load_workbooks()
            result = self.compare_sheets()
            if output_path is None:
                output_path = str(Path(self._project_workbook_path).with_name(self.report_name))
            result.output_file = self.save_report(result, output_path)
            return result
        finally:
            self.close_workbooks()

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Mapping

from openpyxl.utils.cell import coordinate_from_string
from openpyxl.utils.exceptions import CellCoordinatesException


class DataValidationError(ValueError):
    pass


@dataclass
class TrainTypeValues:
    train_type: str
    values: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def get_value(self, key: str) -> Any:
        return self.values.get(key)

    @classmethod
    def from_mapping(cls, train_type: str, raw_values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> "TrainTypeValues":
        if not isinstance(train_type, str) or not train_type.strip():
            raise DataValidationError("Train type name must be a non-empty string.")
        if not isinstance(raw_values, Mapping):
            raise DataValidationError(f"Train type '{train_type}' must map to an object.")

        normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for key, value in raw_values.items():
            if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
                raise DataValidationError(
                    f"Train type '{train_type}' contains an invalid field name: {key!r}."
                )
            normalized[key] = value

        return cls(train_type=train_type, values=normalized)


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ExcelMappingEntry:
    sheet: str
    cell: str
    field: str

    @classmethod
    def from_mapping(cls, raw_entry: Mapping[str, Any]) -> "ExcelMappingEntry":
        if not isinstance(raw_entry, Mapping):
            raise DataValidationError("Excel mapping entries must be objects.")

        sheet = raw_entry.get("sheet", "")
        cell = raw_entry.get("cell", "")
        field = raw_entry.get("field", "")

        if not isinstance(sheet, str) or not sheet.strip():
            raise DataValidationError("Excel mapping entry is missing a valid 'sheet'.")
        if not isinstance(cell, str) or not cell.strip():
            raise DataValidationError(f"Excel mapping entry for sheet '{sheet}' is missing a valid 'cell'.")
        if not isinstance(field, str) or not field.strip():
            raise DataValidationError(f"Excel mapping entry for sheet '{sheet}' is missing a valid 'field'.")

        try:
            coordinate_from_string(cell)
        except (ValueError, CellCoordinatesException) as exc:
            raise DataValidationError(
                f"Excel mapping entry for sheet '{sheet}' uses an invalid cell reference: {cell!r}."
            ) from exc

        return cls(sheet=sheet, cell=cell, field=field)

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Any


class BaseRepository(ABC):

    @abstractmethod
    def load_train_type_values(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return the full train_type_values structure as a dict."""

    @abstractmethod
    def load_excel_mapping(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return the excel_mapping structure as a dict."""
