import json
import logging
from typing import Any

from app.config import TRAIN_TYPE_VALUES_FILE, EXCEL_MAPPING_FILE
from app.repositories.base_repository import BaseRepository

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class LocalJsonRepository(BaseRepository):

    def _load_file(self, path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        logger.info("Loading local file: %s", path)
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8-sig") as fh:
            return json.load(fh)

    def load_train_type_values(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self._load_file(TRAIN_TYPE_VALUES_FILE)

    def load_excel_mapping(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self._load_file(EXCEL_MAPPING_FILE)

FILE: app\repositories\sharepoint_repository.py | SIZE: 1.97 KB | ENCODING: utf-8-sig

import json
import logging
from typing import Any

import urllib.request
import urllib.error

from app.config import (
    SHAREPOINT_TRAIN_TYPE_VALUES_URL,
    SHAREPOINT_EXCEL_MAPPING_URL,
    REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
)
from app.repositories.base_repository import BaseRepository
from app.repositories.local_json_repository import LocalJsonRepository

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class SharePointRepository(BaseRepository):

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._local_fallback = LocalJsonRepository()

    def _fetch_url(self, url: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        logger.info("Attempting to fetch from SharePoint: %s", url)
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"Accept": "application/json"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) as response:
            raw = response.read()
        data = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
        logger.info("Successfully loaded from SharePoint: %s", url)
        return data

    def _fetch_with_fallback(
        self,
        url: str,
        local_loader,
        label: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            data = self._fetch_url(url)
            logger.info("Source used for %s: SharePoint", label)
            return data
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning(
                "SharePoint unavailable for %s (%s). Falling back to local.", label, exc
            )
            data = local_loader()
            logger.info("Source used for %s: local JSON", label)
            return data

    def load_train_type_values(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self._fetch_with_fallback(
            SHAREPOINT_TRAIN_TYPE_VALUES_URL,
            self._local_fallback.load_train_type_values,
            "train_type_values",
        )

    def load_excel_mapping(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self._fetch_with_fallback(
            SHAREPOINT_EXCEL_MAPPING_URL,
            self._local_fallback.load_excel_mapping,
            "excel_mapping",
        )

FILE: app\services\data_update_service.py | SIZE: 2.16 KB | ENCODING: utf-8-sig

import json
import logging
import os
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

from app.config import METADATA_FILE

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _default_metadata() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "schema_version": "1.0",
        "description": "Train Type Data Preparation Tool metadata",
        "generation_history": [],
    }


class DataUpdateService:

    def get_metadata(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not os.path.exists(METADATA_FILE):
            logger.warning("metadata.json not found at: %s", METADATA_FILE)
            return _default_metadata()
        with open(METADATA_FILE, encoding="utf-8-sig") as fh:
            metadata = json.load(fh)
        if not isinstance(metadata, dict):
            return _default_metadata()
        metadata.setdefault("schema_version", "1.0")
        metadata.setdefault("description", "Train Type Data Preparation Tool metadata")
        metadata.setdefault("generation_history", [])
        return metadata

    def record_generation_event(self, train_type: str, output_path: str) -> None:
        metadata = self.get_metadata()
        history: list = metadata.setdefault("generation_history", [])
        history.append(
            {
                "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                "train_type": train_type,
                "output_path": output_path,
            }
        )
        target_dir = os.path.dirname(METADATA_FILE)
        os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)
        temp_path = ""
        try:
            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", encoding="utf-8", delete=False, dir=target_dir) as temp_file:
                temp_path = temp_file.name
                json.dump(metadata, temp_file, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
            os.replace(temp_path, METADATA_FILE)
            logger.info("Recorded generation event for train type '%s'.", train_type)
        finally:
            if temp_path and os.path.exists(temp_path):
                try:
                    os.remove(temp_path)
                except OSError:
                    logger.debug("Temporary metadata file cleanup failed for %s", temp_path, exc_info=True)

FILE: app\services\excel_generator.py | SIZE: 6.08 KB | ENCODING: utf-8-sig

import logging
import os
import shutil
from dataclasses import dataclass

import openpyxl

from app.config import TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE
from app.models.train_type import DataValidationError, ExcelMappingEntry, TrainTypeValues
from app.repositories.base_repository import BaseRepository

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ParameterColumnMapping:
    sheet: str
    parameter_column: str
    value_column: str
    start_row: int = 1
    end_row: int | None = None

    @classmethod
    def from_mapping(cls, raw: dict) -> "ParameterColumnMapping":
        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
            raise DataValidationError("The 'parameter_column_mapping' value must be an object.")

        sheet = raw.get("sheet", "")
        parameter_column = raw.get("parameter_column", "")
        value_column = raw.get("value_column", "")
        start_row = raw.get("start_row", 1)
        end_row = raw.get("end_row")

        if not isinstance(sheet, str) or not sheet.strip():
            raise DataValidationError("The parameter_column_mapping requires a valid 'sheet'.")
        if not isinstance(parameter_column, str) or not parameter_column.strip():
            raise DataValidationError("The parameter_column_mapping requires a valid 'parameter_column'.")
        if not isinstance(value_column, str) or not value_column.strip():
            raise DataValidationError("The parameter_column_mapping requires a valid 'value_column'.")
        if not isinstance(start_row, int) or start_row < 1:
            raise DataValidationError("The parameter_column_mapping 'start_row' must be an integer >= 1.")
        if end_row is not None and (not isinstance(end_row, int) or end_row < start_row):
            raise DataValidationError("The parameter_column_mapping 'end_row' must be null or >= start_row.")

        return cls(
            sheet=sheet,
            parameter_column=parameter_column,
            value_column=value_column,
            start_row=start_row,
            end_row=end_row,
        )


class ExcelGenerator:

    def __init__(self, repository: BaseRepository) -> None:
        self._repository = repository
        self._mapping: list[ExcelMappingEntry] | None = None
        self._parameter_column_mapping: ParameterColumnMapping | None = None

    def _ensure_mapping_loaded(self) -> None:
        if self._mapping is None:
            raw = self._repository.load_excel_mapping()
            mappings = raw.get("mappings", [])
            if not isinstance(mappings, list):
                raise DataValidationError("The top-level 'mappings' value must be a list.")
            self._mapping = [ExcelMappingEntry.from_mapping(entry) for entry in mappings]

            parameter_column_mapping = raw.get("parameter_column_mapping")
            if parameter_column_mapping is not None:
                self._parameter_column_mapping = ParameterColumnMapping.from_mapping(parameter_column_mapping)

    def _write_with_explicit_mappings(self, wb, train_type_values: TrainTypeValues) -> None:
        for entry in self._mapping:
            if entry.sheet not in wb.sheetnames:
                logger.warning("Sheet '%s' not found in template — skipping.", entry.sheet)
                continue

            ws = wb[entry.sheet]
            value = train_type_values.get_value(entry.field)
            if value is None:
                logger.warning(
                    "No value for field '%s' (train type '%s') — cell %s!%s left unchanged.",
                    entry.field,
                    train_type_values.train_type,
                    entry.sheet,
                    entry.cell,
                )
                continue

            ws[entry.cell] = value
            logger.debug("Wrote %s -> %s!%s", entry.field, entry.sheet, entry.cell)

    def _write_with_parameter_column_mapping(self, wb, train_type_values: TrainTypeValues) -> None:
        mapping = self._parameter_column_mapping
        if mapping is None:
            return
        if mapping.sheet not in wb.sheetnames:
            raise DataValidationError(
                f"Sheet '{mapping.sheet}' from parameter_column_mapping was not found in template workbook."
            )

        ws = wb[mapping.sheet]
        end_row = mapping.end_row or ws.max_row

        parameter_to_row: dict[str, int] = {}
        for row in range(mapping.start_row, end_row + 1):
            parameter_key = ws[f"{mapping.parameter_column}{row}"].value
            if isinstance(parameter_key, str) and parameter_key.strip():
                parameter_to_row[parameter_key.strip()] = row

        missing_fields: list[str] = []
        for field_key, field_value in train_type_values.values.items():
            row = parameter_to_row.get(field_key)
            if row is None:
                missing_fields.append(field_key)
                continue
            ws[f"{mapping.value_column}{row}"] = field_value

        if missing_fields:
            logger.warning(
                "The following fields were not found in parameter column '%s' for sheet '%s': %s",
                mapping.parameter_column,
                mapping.sheet,
                ", ".join(sorted(missing_fields)),
            )

    def generate(self, train_type_values: TrainTypeValues, output_path: str) -> None:
        self._ensure_mapping_loaded()

        if not os.path.exists(TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE):
            raise FileNotFoundError(
                f"Template not found: {TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE}"
            )

        output_dir = os.path.dirname(output_path)
        if output_dir:
            os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)

        shutil.copy2(TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE, output_path)
        logger.info("Copied template to: %s", output_path)

        wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(output_path)
        try:
            if self._mapping:
                self._write_with_explicit_mappings(wb, train_type_values)
            if self._parameter_column_mapping is not None:
                self._write_with_parameter_column_mapping(wb, train_type_values)

            wb.save(output_path)
            logger.info("Excel file saved: %s", output_path)
        finally:
            wb.close()

FILE: app\services\rebuild.py | SIZE: 3.96 KB | ENCODING: utf-8-sig
========================================================================================================================

import json
import logging
import os
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from openpyxl import Workbook

from app.config import (
    GENERIC_VARIANT_FILE,
    HEAVY_DIESEL_VARIANT_FILE,
    LIGHT_DIESEL_VARIANT_FILE,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TrainTypeVariant:
    display_name: str
    json_path: str


class RebuildService:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._variants: dict[str, TrainTypeVariant] = {
            "Generic": TrainTypeVariant("Generic", GENERIC_VARIANT_FILE),
            "Heavy Diesel": TrainTypeVariant("Heavy Diesel", HEAVY_DIESEL_VARIANT_FILE),
            "Light Diesel": TrainTypeVariant("Light Diesel", LIGHT_DIESEL_VARIANT_FILE),
        }

    def get_available_train_types(self) -> list[str]:
        return list(self._variants.keys())

    def _load_variant_json(self, train_type: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        variant = self._variants.get(train_type)
        if variant is None:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown train type: '{train_type}'")

        if not os.path.exists(variant.json_path):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Variant file not found: {variant.json_path}")

        logger.info("Loading train type variant from: %s", variant.json_path)
        with open(variant.json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8-sig") as file_obj:
            data = json.load(file_obj)

        required_keys = {"sheet_name", "cells", "column_widths", "row_heights", "merged_cells"}
        missing = required_keys - set(data.keys())
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Variant file '{variant.json_path}' is missing required keys: {', '.join(sorted(missing))}"
            )

        self._validate_variant_cells(variant.json_path, data)

        return data

    @staticmethod
    def _looks_like_serialized_openpyxl_object(value: Any) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(value, str):
            return False
        return value.startswith("<openpyxl.") or re.search(r" object at 0x[0-9A-Fa-f]+>", value) is not None

    def _validate_variant_cells(self, variant_path: str, data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        cells = data.get("cells", {})
        if not isinstance(cells, dict):
            return

        for address, cell_data in cells.items():
            if not isinstance(cell_data, dict):
                continue
            for field_name, field_value in cell_data.items():
                if self._looks_like_serialized_openpyxl_object(field_value):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Variant file '{variant_path}' contains invalid serialized object text at cell {address} field '{field_name}'."
                    )

    def generate_excel(self, train_type: str, output_path: str) -> None:
        data = self._load_variant_json(train_type)

        output_dir = os.path.dirname(output_path)
        if output_dir:
            os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)

        workbook = Workbook()
        worksheet = workbook.active
        worksheet.title = data["sheet_name"]

        for address, cell_data in data["cells"].items():
            worksheet[address] = cell_data.get("value")

        for col, width in data["column_widths"].items():
            if width is not None:
                worksheet.column_dimensions[col].width = width

        for row_num, height in data["row_heights"].items():
            if height is not None:
                worksheet.row_dimensions[int(row_num)].height = height

        for merged_range in data["merged_cells"]:
            try:
                worksheet.merge_cells(merged_range)
            except Exception:
                logger.warning("Merge error for range %s", merged_range, exc_info=True)

        try:
            workbook.save(output_path)
            logger.info("Saved rebuilt workbook for '%s' to: %s", train_type, output_path)
        finally:
            workbook.close()

FILE: app\services\train_type_service.py | SIZE: 1.29 KB | ENCODING: utf-8-sig

import logging
from typing import Any

from app.models.train_type import DataValidationError, TrainTypeValues
from app.repositories.base_repository import BaseRepository

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class TrainTypeService:

    def __init__(self, repository: BaseRepository) -> None:
        self._repository = repository
        self._raw: dict[str, Any] | None = None

    def _ensure_loaded(self) -> None:
        if self._raw is None:
            self._raw = self._repository.load_train_type_values()

    def get_available_train_types(self) -> list[str]:
        self._ensure_loaded()
        train_types = self._raw.get("train_types", {})
        if not isinstance(train_types, dict):
            raise DataValidationError("The top-level 'train_types' value must be an object.")
        return list(train_types.keys())

    def get_train_type_values(self, train_type: str) -> TrainTypeValues:
        self._ensure_loaded()
        all_types: dict = self._raw.get("train_types", {})
        if not isinstance(all_types, dict):
            raise DataValidationError("The top-level 'train_types' value must be an object.")
        if train_type not in all_types:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown train type: '{train_type}'")
        return TrainTypeValues.from_mapping(train_type, all_types[train_type])

class CompareTab:
    _PREVIEW_LIMIT = 20

    def __init__(self, parent: ttk.Frame) -> None:

        self._root = parent.winfo_toplevel()

        self._cells_text = tk.StringVar(value="-")
        self._raw_deviations_text = tk.StringVar(value="-")
        self._actionable_deviations_text = tk.StringVar(value="-")
        self._time_text = tk.StringVar(value="-")
        self._output_text = tk.StringVar(value="-")

        self._preview_count_text = tk.StringVar(value="-")
        self._preview_note_text = tk.StringVar(

        self._raw_count_for_preview = 0

        try:

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Failed to load standard metadata.")
            self._metadata = None
            self._report_generator = None
            self._metadata_error = f"Standard metadata could not be loaded from {STANDARD_7_7_2_METADATA_FILE}: {exc}"
            self._standard_display_text.set("Unavailable")
            self._warning_text.set(self._metadata_error)

        self._build_ui()
        if self._metadata is None:
            self._generate_btn.configure(state="disabled")

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:

        self._parent.rowconfigure(0, weight=1)

        ttk.Entry(container, textvariable=self._project_path).grid(row=2, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(8, 8), pady=4)

        self._add_summary_row(summary, 5, "Output File", self._output_text)

        ttk.Label(preview, textvariable=self._preview_note_text, wraplength=760, justify="left").grid(

            fg="#333333",
            bg=self._root.cget("bg"),
        )
        self._status_label.grid(row=7, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="ew")

    @staticmethod
    def _add_summary_row(parent, row: int, label_text: str, variable: tk.StringVar) -> None:
        ttk.Label(parent, text=f"{label_text}:").grid(row=row, column=0, sticky="w", pady=2)
        ttk.Label(parent, textvariable=variable).grid(row=row, column=1, sticky="w", pady=2)

    def _browse_project(self) -> None:
        file_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            title="Select Project Workbook",
            filetypes=[("Excel Macro-Enabled Workbook", "*.xlsm"), ("Excel Workbook", "*.xlsx"), ("All files", "*.*")],
        )
        if file_path:
            self._project_path.set(file_path)

    def _set_status(self, message: str) -> None:
        self._status_text.set(message)
        self._root.update_idletasks()

    def _clear_summary(self) -> None:
        self._sheets_text.set("-")
        self._cells_text.set("-")
        self._raw_deviations_text.set("-")
        self._actionable_deviations_text.set("-")
        self._time_text.set("-")
        self._output_text.set("-")

    def _clear_preview(self) -> None:
        self._preview_title_text.set("Preview of actionable deviations")
        self._preview_count_text.set("-")
        self._preview_note_text.set(
            "Run a comparison to preview the actionable deviations before saving the report."
        )
        self._preview_rows = []
        for item in self._preview_tree.get_children():
            self._preview_tree.delete(item)

    def _populate_preview(self, deviations) -> None:
        preview_rows = deviations[: self._PREVIEW_LIMIT]
        self._preview_rows = [
            (
                item.sheet_name,
                item.cell_address,
                "" if item.standard_value is None else str(item.standard_value),
                "" if item.project_value is None else str(item.project_value),
                item.parameter,
                item.rule,
            )
            for item in preview_rows
        ]

        self._preview_title_text.set("Preview of actionable deviations")
        self._preview_count_text.set(
            f"Showing {len(self._preview_rows)} actionable deviations of {len(deviations)} actionable from {self._raw_count_for_preview} raw differences."
        )
        if deviations:
            self._preview_note_text.set(
                "Review the preview below. The report will be saved only after you confirm the file location."
            )
        else:
            self._preview_note_text.set("No actionable deviations were found. A report can still be saved if needed.")

        for item in self._preview_tree.get_children():
            self._preview_tree.delete(item)

        for row in self._preview_rows:
            self._preview_tree.insert("", "end", values=row)

    def _start_comparison(self, project_path: str) -> None:
        if self._comparison_running:
            messagebox.showwarning("Comparison in progress", "Please wait for the current comparison to finish.")
            return

        self._clear_summary()
        self._clear_preview()
        self._comparison_queue = queue.Queue()
        self._comparison_thread = threading.Thread(
            target=run_comparison_worker,
            args=(project_path, self._metadata, self._comparison_queue),
            daemon=True,
        )
        self._comparison_running = True
        self._comparison_started_at = time.perf_counter()
        self._generate_btn.configure(state="disabled")
        self._set_status("Workbook loading...")
        self._comparison_thread.start()
        self._root.after(100, self._poll_comparison_queue)

    def _poll_comparison_queue(self) -> None:
        if not self._comparison_running or self._comparison_queue is None:
            return

        while True:
            try:
                event = self._comparison_queue.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                break

            if isinstance(event, ComparisonWorkerProgress):
                self._set_status(event.message)
                continue

            if isinstance(event, ComparisonWorkerSuccess):
                self._handle_comparison_success(event.result)
                return

            if isinstance(event, ComparisonWorkerFailure):
                self._handle_comparison_failure(event)
                return

        if self._comparison_running:
            self._root.after(100, self._poll_comparison_queue)

    def _finish_comparison_session(self) -> None:
        self._comparison_running = False
        self._comparison_queue = None
        self._comparison_thread = None
        self._generate_btn.configure(state="normal")

    def _handle_comparison_failure(self, event: ComparisonWorkerFailure) -> None:
        try:
            logger.error(
                "Comparison failed in worker: %s",
                event.exception,
                exc_info=(type(event.exception), event.exception, event.exception.__traceback__),
            )
            if event.error_kind == "standard_metadata":
                self._set_status(f"Error: {event.exception}")
                messagebox.showerror(
                    "Comparison Failed",
                    f"Standard metadata could not be loaded:\n{STANDARD_7_7_2_METADATA_FILE}\n\n{event.exception}",
                )
            elif event.error_kind == "workbook_load":
                self._set_status(f"Error: {event.exception}")
                messagebox.showerror("Workbook Loading Failed", str(event.exception))
            elif event.error_kind == "comparison":
                self._set_status(f"Error: {event.exception}")
                messagebox.showerror("Comparison Failed", str(event.exception))
            else:
                self._set_status(f"Unexpected error: {event.exception}")
                messagebox.showerror("Comparison Failed", str(event.exception))
        finally:
            self._finish_comparison_session()

    def _handle_comparison_success(self, result) -> None:
        raw_count = len(result.deviations)
        actionable_deviations = [deviation for deviation in result.deviations if is_actionable(deviation)]
        compare_elapsed = result.summary.execution_time_seconds or round(time.perf_counter() - self._comparison_started_at, 2)

        self._sheets_text.set(str(result.summary.total_sheets_compared))
        self._cells_text.set(str(result.summary.total_cells_compared))
        self._raw_deviations_text.set(str(raw_count))
        self._actionable_deviations_text.set(str(len(actionable_deviations)))
        self._time_text.set(f"Comparison: {compare_elapsed:.2f} s")
        self._output_text.set("Not saved yet")
        self._raw_count_for_preview = raw_count
        self._populate_preview(actionable_deviations)

        try:
            should_save = messagebox.askyesno(
                "Save Deviation Report",
                f"Comparison finished with {len(actionable_deviations)} actionable deviations from {raw_count} raw differences.\n\n"
                "Do you want to save the deviation report now?",
            )
            if not should_save:
                self._set_status("Comparison completed. Report was not saved.")
                return

            output_path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
                title="Save deviation report",
                defaultextension=".xlsx",
                filetypes=[("Excel files", "*.xlsx")],
                initialfile=self._metadata.report_name,
            )
            if not output_path:
                self._set_status("Report save cancelled.")
                return

            self._set_status("Creating deviation report...")
            save_start = time.perf_counter()
            result.output_file = self._report_generator.generate(result, output_path)
            total_elapsed = round(time.perf_counter() - self._comparison_started_at, 2)
            save_elapsed = round(time.perf_counter() - save_start, 2)
            result.summary.execution_time_seconds = total_elapsed

            self._sheets_text.set(str(result.summary.total_sheets_compared))
            self._cells_text.set(str(result.summary.total_cells_compared))
            self._raw_deviations_text.set(str(raw_count))
            self._actionable_deviations_text.set(str(len(actionable_deviations)))
            self._time_text.set(f"Total: {total_elapsed:.2f} s (save: {save_elapsed:.2f} s)")
            self._output_text.set(result.output_file)
            self._set_status("Comparison completed successfully.")
        except ComparisonError as exc:
            logger.exception("Report generation failed.")
            self._set_status(f"Error: {exc}")
            messagebox.showerror("Comparison Failed", str(exc))
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Unexpected report save failure.")
            self._set_status(f"Unexpected error: {exc}")
            messagebox.showerror("Comparison Failed", str(exc))
        finally:
            self._finish_comparison_session()

    def _on_generate(self) -> None:
        if self._metadata is None:
            messagebox.showerror("Comparison unavailable", self._metadata_error or "Standard workbook metadata could not be loaded.")
            return

        project_path = self._project_path.get().strip()
        if not project_path:
            messagebox.showwarning("Missing File", "Please select the project workbook first.")
            return

        if not os.path.exists(project_path):
            messagebox.showerror("Missing File", f"Project workbook not found:\n{project_path}")
            return

        self._start_comparison(project_path)

FILE: app\ui\main_window.py | SIZE: 4.63 KB | ENCODING: utf-8-sig

import logging
import os
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox, ttk

from app.ui.compare_tab import CompareTab

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_STATUS_IDLE = ""
_COLOR_SUCCESS = "#1a7a1a"
_COLOR_ERROR = "#b00000"
_COLOR_IDLE = "#333333"


class MainWindow:

    def __init__(
        self,
        root: tk.Tk,
        rebuild_service,
        data_update_service,
    ) -> None:
        self._root = root
        self._rebuild_service = rebuild_service
        self._data_update_service = data_update_service

        self._selected_train_type = tk.StringVar()
        self._status_text = tk.StringVar(value=_STATUS_IDLE)
        self._status_color = _COLOR_IDLE

        self._build_ui()
        self._load_train_types()

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        self._root.title("Train Type Data Preparation Tool")
        self._root.resizable(True, True)
        self._root.minsize(980, 640)

        notebook = ttk.Notebook(self._root)
        notebook.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        generate_frame = ttk.Frame(notebook, padding=20)
        compare_frame = ttk.Frame(notebook, padding=20)
        notebook.add(generate_frame, text="Generate Train Type")
        notebook.add(compare_frame, text="Compare Workbook")

        self._root.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self._root.rowconfigure(0, weight=1)

        ttk.Label(generate_frame, text="Select Train Type:").grid(
            row=0, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(0, 4)
        )
        self._combo = ttk.Combobox(
            generate_frame,
            textvariable=self._selected_train_type,
            state="readonly",
            width=35,
        )
        self._combo.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew", pady=(0, 16))

        self._generate_btn = ttk.Button(
            generate_frame,
            text="Generate Excel",
            command=self._on_generate,
        )
        self._generate_btn.grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="ew", pady=(0, 12))

        self._status_label = tk.Label(
            generate_frame,
            textvariable=self._status_text,
            wraplength=380,
            justify="left",
            fg=_COLOR_IDLE,
            bg=self._root.cget("bg"),
        )
        self._status_label.grid(row=3, column=0, sticky="ew")
        generate_frame.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

        self._compare_tab = CompareTab(compare_frame)

    def _load_train_types(self) -> None:
        try:
            types = self._rebuild_service.get_available_train_types()
            self._combo["values"] = types
            if types:
                self._combo.current(0)
            self._set_status("Data loaded successfully.", success=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Failed to load train types.")
            self._set_status(f"Error loading train types: {exc}", success=False)
            self._generate_btn.configure(state="disabled")

    def _on_generate(self) -> None:
        train_type = self._selected_train_type.get().strip()
        if not train_type:
            messagebox.showwarning("No Selection", "Please select a train type first.")
            return

        output_path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            title="Save generated Excel file",
            defaultextension=".xlsx",
            filetypes=[("Excel files", "*.xlsx")],
            initialfile=f"{train_type.replace(' ', '')}.xlsx",
        )
        if not output_path:
            self._set_status("Generation cancelled.", success=None)
            return

        self._generate_btn.configure(state="disabled")
        self._set_status("Generating…", success=None)
        self._root.update_idletasks()

        try:
            self._rebuild_service.generate_excel(train_type, output_path)
            self._data_update_service.record_generation_event(train_type, output_path)
            filename = os.path.basename(output_path)
            self._set_status(f"Success! File saved: {filename}", success=True)
            logger.info("Generated Excel for '%s' at '%s'.", train_type, output_path)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Excel generation failed.")
            self._set_status(f"Error: {exc}", success=False)
            messagebox.showerror("Generation failed", str(exc))
        finally:
            self._generate_btn.configure(state="normal")

    def _set_status(self, message: str, success: bool | None) -> None:
        self._status_text.set(message)
        if success is True:
            color = _COLOR_SUCCESS
        elif success is False:
            color = _COLOR_ERROR
        else:
            color = _COLOR_IDLE
        self._status_label.configure(fg=color)

    def run(self) -> None:
        self._root.mainloop()

"""
Run this script once to create templates/train_type_template.xlsx.

Usage:
    python legacy/developer_utility/create_template.py
"""
import os

import openpyxl
from openpyxl.styles import Alignment, Font, PatternFill

TEMPLATES_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__))), "templates")
OUTPUT_PATH = os.path.join(TEMPLATES_DIR, "train_type_template.xlsx")

HEADER_FILL = PatternFill(start_color="1F4E79", end_color="1F4E79", fill_type="solid")
HEADER_FONT = Font(color="FFFFFF", bold=True, size=11)
LABEL_FONT = Font(bold=True, size=10)
LABEL_FILL = PatternFill(start_color="D6E4F0", end_color="D6E4F0", fill_type="solid")
CENTER = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center")

ROWS = [
    ("A1", "Parameter", "B1", "Value"),
    ("A2", "Max Speed (km/h)", "B2", ""),
    ("A3", "Traction Type", "B3", ""),
    ("A4", "Axle Load (t)", "B4", ""),
    ("A5", "Length (m)", "B5", ""),
    ("A6", "Passenger Capacity", "B6", ""),
    ("A7", "Power (kW)", "B7", ""),
    ("A8", "Braking Distance (m)", "B8", ""),
    ("A9", "Bogies Count", "B9", ""),
    ("A10", "Gauge (mm)", "B10", ""),
    ("A11", "Notes", "B11", ""),
]


def build_template() -> None:
    os.makedirs(TEMPLATES_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
    ws = wb.active
    ws.title = "Train Type"

    ws.column_dimensions["A"].width = 28
    ws.column_dimensions["B"].width = 40

    for label_cell, label_text, value_cell, value_text in ROWS:
        lc = ws[label_cell]
        lc.value = label_text
        lc.alignment = CENTER

        vc = ws[value_cell]
        vc.value = value_text
        vc.alignment = CENTER

        if label_cell == "A1":
            lc.font = HEADER_FONT
            lc.fill = HEADER_FILL
            vc.font = HEADER_FONT
            vc.fill = HEADER_FILL
        else:
            lc.font = LABEL_FONT
            lc.fill = LABEL_FILL

    wb.save(OUTPUT_PATH)
    wb.close()
    print(f"Template created: {OUTPUT_PATH}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    build_template()

import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
import json
import queue
from unittest import mock

import openpyxl

from app.comparison.deviation_policy import is_actionable, is_placeholder
from app.comparison.compare_worker import (
    ComparisonWorkerFailure,
    ComparisonWorkerProgress,
    ComparisonWorkerSuccess,
    run_comparison_worker,
)
from app.comparison.models import ComparisonSummary, DeviationRecord, WorkbookComparisonResult
from app.comparison.passive_rules_parser import PassiveRulesParser
from app.comparison.report_generator import ReportGenerator
from app.comparison.standard_metadata import StandardWorkbookMetadata
from app.comparison.workbook_comparator import WorkbookComparator
from app.config import STANDARD_7_7_2_METADATA_FILE, STANDARD_7_7_2_WORKBOOK
from app.models.train_type import DataValidationError, ExcelMappingEntry, TrainTypeValues
from app.services.data_update_service import DataUpdateService
from app.services.rebuild import RebuildService, TrainTypeVariant
from app.services.excel_generator import ExcelGenerator
from app.services.train_type_service import TrainTypeService
from app.comparison.models import WorkbookLoadError


class FakeRepository:
    def __init__(self, train_type_values: dict | None = None, excel_mapping: dict | None = None) -> None:
        self._train_type_values = train_type_values or {}
        self._excel_mapping = excel_mapping or {}

    def load_train_type_values(self) -> dict:
        return self._train_type_values

    def load_excel_mapping(self) -> dict:
        return self._excel_mapping


class ModelValidationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_train_type_values_from_mapping_rejects_invalid_structure(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(DataValidationError):
            TrainTypeValues.from_mapping("train-a", {1: "invalid"})  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    def test_excel_mapping_entry_validates_cell_reference(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(DataValidationError):
            ExcelMappingEntry.from_mapping({"sheet": "Train Type", "cell": "bad-cell", "field": "speed"})


class ServiceAndGeneratorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_train_type_service_returns_available_types_and_values(self) -> None:
        repository = FakeRepository(
            train_type_values={"train_types": {"generic": {"max_speed_kmh": 160}}},
            excel_mapping={"mappings": []},
        )
        service = TrainTypeService(repository)

        self.assertEqual(service.get_available_train_types(), ["generic"])
        values = service.get_train_type_values("generic")
        self.assertEqual(values.get_value("max_speed_kmh"), 160)

    def test_excel_generator_writes_values_into_template(self) -> None:
        repository = FakeRepository(
            train_type_values={"train_types": {"generic": {"max_speed_kmh": 160}}},
            excel_mapping={
                "mappings": [
                    {"sheet": "Train Type", "cell": "B2", "field": "max_speed_kmh"}
                ]
            },
        )

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            template_path = temp_path / "template.xlsx"
            output_path = temp_path / "output.xlsx"

            workbook = openpyxl.Workbook()
            worksheet = workbook.active
            worksheet.title = "Train Type"
            worksheet["B2"] = None
            workbook.save(template_path)

            import app.services.excel_generator as excel_generator_module

            original_template_file = excel_generator_module.TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE
            excel_generator_module.TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE = str(template_path)
            try:
                generator = ExcelGenerator(repository)
                train_type_values = TrainTypeValues.from_mapping("generic", {"max_speed_kmh": 160})
                generator.generate(train_type_values, str(output_path))
            finally:
                excel_generator_module.TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE = original_template_file

            generated_workbook = openpyxl.load_workbook(output_path)
            try:
                self.assertEqual(generated_workbook["Train Type"]["B2"].value, 160)
            finally:
                generated_workbook.close()

    def test_excel_generator_writes_values_using_parameter_column_mapping(self) -> None:
        repository = FakeRepository(
            train_type_values={"train_types": {"generic": {"train_type_name": "GENERIC_X"}}},
            excel_mapping={
                "mappings": [],
                "parameter_column_mapping": {
                    "sheet": "Train type (2)",
                    "parameter_column": "D",
                    "value_column": "E",
                    "start_row": 1,
                },
            },
        )

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            template_path = temp_path / "template.xlsx"
            output_path = temp_path / "output.xlsx"

            workbook = openpyxl.Workbook()
            worksheet = workbook.active
            worksheet.title = "Train type (2)"
            worksheet["D11"] = "train_type_name"
            worksheet["E11"] = "OLD"
            workbook.save(template_path)

            import app.services.excel_generator as excel_generator_module

            original_template_file = excel_generator_module.TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE
            excel_generator_module.TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE = str(template_path)
            try:
                generator = ExcelGenerator(repository)
                train_type_values = TrainTypeValues.from_mapping("generic", {"train_type_name": "GENERIC_X"})
                generator.generate(train_type_values, str(output_path))
            finally:
                excel_generator_module.TRAIN_TYPE_TEMPLATE_FILE = original_template_file

            generated_workbook = openpyxl.load_workbook(output_path)
            try:
                self.assertEqual(generated_workbook["Train type (2)"]["E11"].value, "GENERIC_X")
            finally:
                generated_workbook.close()


class MetadataAndPersistenceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_embedded_standard_metadata_matches_workbook_filename(self) -> None:
        metadata = StandardWorkbookMetadata.load(STANDARD_7_7_2_METADATA_FILE)

        self.assertTrue(Path(STANDARD_7_7_2_WORKBOOK).exists())
        self.assertEqual(Path(STANDARD_7_7_2_WORKBOOK).name, metadata.workbook_file)
        self.assertEqual(Path(metadata.workbook_path).name, Path(STANDARD_7_7_2_WORKBOOK).name)

    def test_data_update_service_writes_valid_json_atomically(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            metadata_file = temp_path / "metadata.json"
            metadata_file.write_text(
                json.dumps({
                    "schema_version": "1.0",
                    "description": "Train Type Data Preparation Tool metadata",
                    "generation_history": [],
                }),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )

            import app.services.data_update_service as data_update_module

            original_metadata_file = data_update_module.METADATA_FILE
            data_update_module.METADATA_FILE = str(metadata_file)
            try:
                service = DataUpdateService()
                service.record_generation_event("Generic", "C:/Temp/output.xlsx")

                with metadata_file.open(encoding="utf-8") as file_obj:
                    metadata = json.load(file_obj)

                self.assertEqual(metadata["schema_version"], "1.0")
                self.assertEqual(metadata["description"], "Train Type Data Preparation Tool metadata")
                self.assertEqual(len(metadata["generation_history"]), 1)
                self.assertEqual(metadata["generation_history"][0]["train_type"], "Generic")
                self.assertEqual(metadata["generation_history"][0]["output_path"], "C:/Temp/output.xlsx")
                self.assertEqual(len(list(temp_path.iterdir())), 1)
            finally:
                data_update_module.METADATA_FILE = original_metadata_file

    def test_data_update_service_cleans_temporary_file_on_replace_failure(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            metadata_file = temp_path / "metadata.json"
            metadata_file.write_text(
                json.dumps({
                    "schema_version": "1.0",
                    "description": "Train Type Data Preparation Tool metadata",
                    "generation_history": [],
                }),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )

            import app.services.data_update_service as data_update_module

            original_metadata_file = data_update_module.METADATA_FILE
            data_update_module.METADATA_FILE = str(metadata_file)
            try:
                service = DataUpdateService()
                with mock.patch.object(data_update_module.os, "replace", side_effect=OSError("replace failed")):
                    with self.assertRaises(OSError):
                        service.record_generation_event("Generic", "C:/Temp/output.xlsx")

                with metadata_file.open(encoding="utf-8") as file_obj:
                    metadata = json.load(file_obj)

                self.assertEqual(metadata["generation_history"], [])
                self.assertEqual(list(temp_path.iterdir()), [metadata_file])
            finally:
                data_update_module.METADATA_FILE = original_metadata_file


class ComparisonWorkerTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_worker_posts_success_and_progress_messages(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            metadata = StandardWorkbookMetadata(
                standard_id="test_standard",
                display_name="Test Standard",
                baseline="7.7.2",
                train_type="Generic",
                workbook_file="standard.xlsm",
                read_only=True,
                precalculated=True,
                password="",
                compare={},
                passive_rules={},
                report_columns=[],
                user_warning="",
                source_path=str(temp_path / "standard_metadata.json"),
            )

            class FakeComparator:
                last_instance = None

                def __init__(self, project_workbook_path: str, metadata: StandardWorkbookMetadata, progress_callback) -> None:
                    self.project_workbook_path = project_workbook_path
                    self.metadata = metadata
                    self.progress_callback = progress_callback
                    self.closed = False
                    FakeComparator.last_instance = self
                    self.progress_callback("Loading standard workbook...")

                def compare_sheets(self) -> WorkbookComparisonResult:
                    self.progress_callback("Comparing cells...")
                    return WorkbookComparisonResult(
                        deviations=[],
                        summary=ComparisonSummary(total_sheets_compared=1, total_cells_compared=2, total_deviations_found=0),
                    )

                def close_workbooks(self) -> None:
                    self.closed = True

            message_queue: queue.Queue = queue.Queue()
            run_comparison_worker("project.xlsm", metadata, message_queue, comparator_factory=FakeComparator)

            messages = []
            while not message_queue.empty():
                messages.append(message_queue.get_nowait())

            self.assertIsInstance(messages[0], ComparisonWorkerProgress)
            self.assertEqual(messages[0].message, "Loading standard workbook...")
            self.assertIsInstance(messages[1], ComparisonWorkerProgress)
            self.assertEqual(messages[1].message, "Comparing cells...")
            self.assertIsInstance(messages[-1], ComparisonWorkerSuccess)
            self.assertEqual(messages[-1].result.summary.total_cells_compared, 2)
            self.assertTrue(FakeComparator.last_instance.closed)

    def test_worker_posts_failure_message_and_closes_workbooks(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            metadata = StandardWorkbookMetadata(
                standard_id="test_standard",
                display_name="Test Standard",
                baseline="7.7.2",
                train_type="Generic",
                workbook_file="standard.xlsm",
                read_only=True,
                precalculated=True,
                password="",
                compare={},
                passive_rules={},
                report_columns=[],
                user_warning="",
                source_path=str(temp_path / "standard_metadata.json"),
            )

            class FakeComparator:
                last_instance = None

                def __init__(self, project_workbook_path: str, metadata: StandardWorkbookMetadata, progress_callback) -> None:
                    self.closed = False
                    FakeComparator.last_instance = self

                def compare_sheets(self):
                    raise WorkbookLoadError("failed to load workbook")

                def close_workbooks(self) -> None:
                    self.closed = True

            message_queue: queue.Queue = queue.Queue()
            run_comparison_worker("project.xlsm", metadata, message_queue, comparator_factory=FakeComparator)

            messages = []
            while not message_queue.empty():
                messages.append(message_queue.get_nowait())

            self.assertIsInstance(messages[-1], ComparisonWorkerFailure)
            self.assertEqual(messages[-1].error_kind, "workbook_load")
            self.assertIsInstance(messages[-1].exception, WorkbookLoadError)
            self.assertTrue(FakeComparator.last_instance.closed)


class RebuildServiceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_rebuild_service_lists_train_types(self) -> None:
        service = RebuildService()
        self.assertEqual(service.get_available_train_types(), ["Generic", "Heavy Diesel", "Light Diesel"])

    def test_rebuild_service_generates_workbook_from_variant_json(self) -> None:
        service = RebuildService()

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            variant_path = temp_path / "variant.json"
            output_path = temp_path / "output.xlsx"

            variant_data = {
                "sheet_name": "IMU",
                "cells": {
                    "A1": {"value": "Header"},
                    "B2": {"value": 123},
                },
                "column_widths": {"A": 22},
                "row_heights": {"1": 30},
                "merged_cells": ["A3:B3"],
            }
            variant_path.write_text(json.dumps(variant_data), encoding="utf-8")

            service._variants = {  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
                "Generic": TrainTypeVariant("Generic", str(variant_path))
            }
            service.generate_excel("Generic", str(output_path))

            workbook = openpyxl.load_workbook(output_path)
            try:
                worksheet = workbook["IMU"]
                self.assertEqual(worksheet["A1"].value, "Header")
                self.assertEqual(worksheet["B2"].value, 123)
                self.assertEqual(worksheet.column_dimensions["A"].width, 22)
                self.assertEqual(worksheet.row_dimensions[1].height, 30)
            finally:
                workbook.close()

    def test_rebuild_service_rejects_serialized_openpyxl_objects(self) -> None:
        service = RebuildService()

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            variant_path = temp_path / "variant.json"
            variant_data = {
                "sheet_name": "IMU",
                "cells": {
                    "A1": {"value": "<openpyxl.cell.cell.Cell object at 0x123>"},
                },
                "column_widths": {},
                "row_heights": {},
                "merged_cells": [],
            }
            variant_path.write_text(json.dumps(variant_data), encoding="utf-8")
            service._variants = {  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
                "Generic": TrainTypeVariant("Generic", str(variant_path))
            }

            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                service._load_variant_json("Generic")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]


class WorkbookComparatorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_comparator_detects_cell_changes_and_missing_sheets(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            standard_path = temp_path / "standard.xlsm"
            project_path = temp_path / "project.xlsm"

            metadata = StandardWorkbookMetadata(
                standard_id="test_standard",
                display_name="Test Standard",
                baseline="7.7.2",
                train_type="Generic",
                workbook_file="standard.xlsm",
                read_only=True,
                precalculated=True,
                password="",
                compare={
                    "ignored_sheets": [],
                    "ignored_prefixes": [],
                    "report_name": "Workbook_Deviations.xlsx",
                    "report_sheet_name": "List of Project Deviations",
                },
                passive_rules={"enabled": False},
                report_columns=[
                    "Change No",
                    "Sheet",
                    "Cell",
                    "Standard Value",
                    "Project Value",
                    "Parameter",
                    "Rule",
                ],
                user_warning="",
                source_path=str(temp_path / "standard_metadata.json"),
            )

            standard_wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
            standard_ws = standard_wb.active
            standard_ws.title = "Visible Sheet"
            standard_ws["A1"] = "keep"
            standard_ws["B2"] = "STANDARD"
            standard_ws["A1"] = "keep"
            standard_extra = standard_wb.create_sheet("Standard Only")
            standard_extra["A1"] = "exists"
            standard_wb.save(standard_path)
            standard_wb.close()

            project_wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
            project_ws = project_wb.active
            project_ws.title = "Visible Sheet"
            project_ws["A1"] = "keep"
            project_ws["B2"] = "PROJECT"
            project_ws["A1"] = "keep"
            project_extra = project_wb.create_sheet("Project Only")
            project_extra["A1"] = "exists"
            project_wb.save(project_path)
            project_wb.close()

            metadata.workbook_file = standard_path.name
            comparator = WorkbookComparator(str(project_path), metadata=metadata)
            result = comparator.generate_report(str(temp_path / "Workbook_Deviations.xlsx"))

            self.assertEqual(result.summary.total_sheets_compared, 1)
            self.assertGreaterEqual(result.summary.total_cells_compared, 2)
            self.assertEqual(result.summary.total_deviations_found, 3)
            self.assertEqual(result.output_file, str(temp_path / "Workbook_Deviations.xlsx"))

            report_wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(result.output_file)
            try:
                self.assertEqual(report_wb.sheetnames, ["Summary", "List of Project Deviations", "All Cell Differences"])

                summary_ws = report_wb["Summary"]
                self.assertEqual(summary_ws["B16"].value, "Sheet")
                self.assertEqual(summary_ws["C16"].value, "Raw differences")
                self.assertEqual(summary_ws["D16"].value, "Actionable differences")
                self.assertEqual(summary_ws["E16"].value, "Passive Rules mapped")

                actionable_ws = report_wb["List of Project Deviations"]
                raw_ws = report_wb["All Cell Differences"]

                self.assertEqual(actionable_ws.title, "List of Project Deviations")
                self.assertEqual(raw_ws.title, "All Cell Differences")

                actionable_rows = list(actionable_ws.iter_rows(min_row=5, values_only=True))
                raw_rows = list(raw_ws.iter_rows(min_row=5, values_only=True))

                self.assertEqual(len(actionable_rows), 3)
                self.assertEqual(len(raw_rows), 3)
                self.assertCountEqual(
                    actionable_rows,
                    [
                        (1, "Project Only", "-", "Missing", "Exists", None, None),
                        (2, "Standard Only", "-", "Exists", "Missing", None, None),
                        (3, "Visible Sheet", "B2", "STANDARD", "PROJECT", None, None),
                    ],
                )
                self.assertCountEqual(raw_rows, actionable_rows)
            finally:
                report_wb.close()


class ReportGeneratorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_report_generator_writes_three_sheet_report(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            metadata = StandardWorkbookMetadata(
                standard_id="test_standard",
                display_name="Test Standard",
                baseline="7.7.2",
                train_type="Generic",
                workbook_file="standard.xlsm",
                read_only=True,
                precalculated=True,
                password="",
                compare={"report_sheet_name": "List of Project Deviations"},
                passive_rules={},
                report_columns=[
                    "Change No",
                    "Sheet",
                    "Cell",
                    "Standard Value",
                    "Project Value",
                    "Parameter",
                    "Rule",
                ],
                user_warning="",
                source_path=str(temp_path / "standard_metadata.json"),
            )
            result = WorkbookComparisonResult(
                deviations=[
                    DeviationRecord(
                        change_type="Value Changed",
                        sheet_name="Train type",
                        cell_address="E412",
                        standard_value="2.0",
                        project_value="2.4",
                        parameter="Param A",
                        rule="Rule 1",
                    )
                ],
                summary=ComparisonSummary(total_sheets_compared=1, total_cells_compared=1, total_deviations_found=1),
                standard_display_name="Test Standard",
                standard_workbook_file="standard.xlsm",
                project_workbook_file="project.xlsm",
            )

            generator = ReportGenerator(metadata)
            output_path = temp_path / "Workbook_Deviations.xlsx"
            generator.generate(result, str(output_path))

            report_wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(output_path)
            try:
                self.assertEqual(report_wb.sheetnames, ["Summary", "List of Project Deviations", "All Cell Differences"])

                summary_ws = report_wb["Summary"]
                self.assertEqual(summary_ws["C5"].value, "Test Standard")
                self.assertEqual(summary_ws["B10"].value, 1)
                self.assertFalse(summary_ws.sheet_view.showGridLines)
                self.assertEqual(summary_ws.freeze_panes, "A8")
                self.assertEqual(summary_ws.column_dimensions["B"].width, 32)
                self.assertEqual(list(summary_ws.tables.keys()), ["SummaryBySheet"])
                self.assertTrue(summary_ws.sheet_properties.tabColor is not None)
                self.assertEqual(summary_ws.sheet_properties.tabColor.rgb[-6:], "1F4E78")

                actionable_ws = report_wb["List of Project Deviations"]
                raw_ws = report_wb["All Cell Differences"]

                actionable_headers = [cell.value for cell in actionable_ws[4]]
                raw_headers = [cell.value for cell in raw_ws[4]]
                expected_headers = ["Change No", "Sheet", "Cell", "Standard Value", "Project Value", "Parameter", "Rule"]
                self.assertEqual(actionable_headers, expected_headers)
                self.assertEqual(raw_headers, expected_headers)
                self.assertEqual(actionable_ws.freeze_panes, "A5")
                self.assertEqual(raw_ws.freeze_panes, "A5")
                self.assertFalse(actionable_ws.sheet_view.showGridLines)
                self.assertFalse(raw_ws.sheet_view.showGridLines)
                self.assertEqual(actionable_ws.column_dimensions["A"].width, 12)
                self.assertEqual(raw_ws.column_dimensions["A"].width, 12)
                self.assertEqual(list(actionable_ws.tables.keys()), ["ActionableDeviationsTable"])
                self.assertEqual(list(raw_ws.tables.keys()), ["AllCellDifferencesTable"])
                self.assertEqual(actionable_ws.sheet_properties.tabColor.rgb[-6:], "70AD47")
                self.assertEqual(raw_ws.sheet_properties.tabColor.rgb[-6:], "A5A5A5")
            finally:
                report_wb.close()

    def test_report_generator_styles_sorted_rows_correctly(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            metadata = StandardWorkbookMetadata(
                standard_id="test_standard",
                display_name="Test Standard",
                baseline="7.7.2",
                train_type="Generic",
                workbook_file="standard.xlsm",
                read_only=True,
                precalculated=True,
                password="",
                compare={"report_sheet_name": "List of Project Deviations"},
                passive_rules={},
                report_columns=[
                    "Change No",
                    "Sheet",
                    "Cell",
                    "Standard Value",
                    "Project Value",
                    "Parameter",
                    "Rule",
                ],
                user_warning="",
                source_path=str(temp_path / "standard_metadata.json"),
            )
            result = WorkbookComparisonResult(
                deviations=[
                    DeviationRecord(
                        change_type="Value Changed",
                        sheet_name="Zeta",
                        cell_address="A1",
                        standard_value="1",
                        project_value="2",
                        parameter="",
                        rule="",
                    ),
                    DeviationRecord(
                        change_type="Value Changed",
                        sheet_name="Alpha",
                        cell_address="A1",
                        standard_value="1",
                        project_value="2",
                        parameter="Param A",
                        rule="Rule 1",
                    ),
                ],
                summary=ComparisonSummary(total_sheets_compared=1, total_cells_compared=2, total_deviations_found=2),
                standard_display_name="Test Standard",
                standard_workbook_file="standard.xlsm",
                project_workbook_file="project.xlsm",
            )

            generator = ReportGenerator(metadata)
            output_path = temp_path / "Workbook_Deviations.xlsx"
            generator.generate(result, str(output_path))

            report_wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(output_path)
            try:
                worksheet = report_wb["List of Project Deviations"]

                def fill_suffix(cell_ref: str) -> str:
                    color = worksheet[cell_ref].fill.fgColor
                    if color.type == "rgb" and color.rgb:
                        return color.rgb[-6:]
                    self.fail(f"Expected rgb fill on {cell_ref}, got {color.type}:{color.value}")

                self.assertEqual(worksheet["B5"].value, "Alpha")
                self.assertEqual(worksheet["B6"].value, "Zeta")
                self.assertEqual(fill_suffix("F5"), "E2F0D9")
                self.assertEqual(fill_suffix("G5"), "E2F0D9")
                self.assertEqual(fill_suffix("D6"), "FFF2CC")
                self.assertEqual(fill_suffix("E6"), "FFF2CC")
            finally:
                report_wb.close()

    def test_deviation_policy_marks_placeholders_and_actionable_rows(self) -> None:
        placeholder = DeviationRecord(
            change_type="Value Changed",
            sheet_name="Train type",
            cell_address="A1",
            standard_value="-",
            project_value="123",
            parameter="",
            rule="",
        )
        actionable = DeviationRecord(
            change_type="Value Changed",
            sheet_name="Train type",
            cell_address="A1",
            standard_value="1",
            project_value="2",
            parameter="Param",
            rule="Rule",
        )

        self.assertTrue(is_placeholder("-"))
        self.assertTrue(is_placeholder(None))
        self.assertFalse(is_placeholder("123"))
        self.assertFalse(is_actionable(placeholder))
        self.assertTrue(is_actionable(actionable))


class PassiveRulesParserTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_passive_rules_parser_maps_parameter_and_rule(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp_path = Path(temp_dir)
            workbook_path = temp_path / "standard.xlsm"
            workbook = openpyxl.Workbook()
            data_sheet = workbook.active
            data_sheet.title = "Train type"
            data_sheet["A1"] = "ANCHOR"
            data_sheet["A2"] = "PARAM_A"
            data_sheet["B2"] = "OLD"
            passive_sheet = workbook.create_sheet("Passive Rules Check")
            passive_sheet["A183"] = "Rule 1"
            passive_sheet["C183"] = "PARAM_A"
            passive_sheet["E183"] = "Train type"
            passive_sheet["F183"] = 0
            passive_sheet["G183"] = 1
            passive_sheet["K183"] = "ANCHOR"
            passive_sheet["L183"] = "Display Param"
            workbook.save(workbook_path)

            metadata = StandardWorkbookMetadata(
                standard_id="test_standard",
                display_name="Test Standard",
                baseline="7.7.2",
                train_type="Generic",
                workbook_file=workbook_path.name,
                read_only=True,
                precalculated=True,
                password="",
                compare={},
                passive_rules={
                    "enabled": True,
                    "sheet_name": "Passive Rules Check",
                    "header_row": 182,
                    "data_start_row": 183,
                    "columns": {
                        "rule": "A",
                        "parameter": "C",
                        "sheet": "E",
                        "row_offset": "F",
                        "column_offset": "G",
                        "find_after": "K",
                        "display_name": "L",
                    },
                },
                report_columns=[],
                user_warning="",
                source_path=str(temp_path / "standard_metadata.json"),
            )

            parser = PassiveRulesParser(metadata)
            workbook_read_only = openpyxl.load_workbook(workbook_path, data_only=True, read_only=True, keep_vba=True)
            try:
                passive_map = parser.build_map(workbook_read_only)
            finally:
                workbook_read_only.close()

            self.assertIn(("Train type", "B2"), passive_map)
            matches = passive_map[("Train type", "B2")]
            self.assertEqual(len(matches), 1)
            self.assertEqual(matches[0].parameter, "Display Param")
            self.assertEqual(matches[0].rule, "Rule 1")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
